/*  Combat - Multi-user spaceship combat.
  84/05/06. A. E. Klietz
  Copyright (c) 1984 by GamBit Multi Systems, Inc.
         All Rights Reserved.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#define COMBAT_PORT  19
extern struct tx_textline  *Text_header;
main()
{
 unsigned tid;
 struct  io_generic_msg io_msg;
 struct  io_endmsg  endmsg;
 word  count = 0;
 TI_TIME  bindate;
 boolean  initial_flag = TRUE;
 
 unbreakable();
 initialize();
 endmsg.io_checkword = IO_CHECKWORD;
 endmsg.io_msgnum = IO_ENDMSG;
 
 for (;;) {
  if (All_mask == 0 && !initial_flag) /* if no active users */
   exit(0);
  breakable();
  if ((tid = receive(0, &io_msg, sizeof(io_msg))) == 0) {
   errlog("main - bad receive.\n");
   continue;
  }
  unbreakable();
  initial_flag = FALSE;
  if (++count % 10 == 0)
   check_hangup();
  update(); /* update the ship positions */
  if (io_msg.io_checkword != IO_CHECKWORD) {
   errlog("main - bad checkword.\n");
   continue;
  }
  endmsg.io_abort = FALSE;
  switch (io_msg.io_msgnum) {
case IO_TEXTMSG:  if (do_textmsg(&io_msg) < 0)
       endmsg.io_abort = TRUE;
      break;
case IO_RESTARTMSG:  if (do_restartmsg(&io_msg) < 0)
       endmsg.io_abort = TRUE;
      break;
case IO_LOGINMSG: check_hangup();
      count = 0;
      do_loginmsg(&io_msg, tid);
      break;
default:  errlog("main - bad io_msgnum.\n");
    continue;
  }
  endmsg.io_ttynum = io_msg.io_ttynum;
  reply(tid, &endmsg, sizeof(endmsg));
 }
}
initialize()
{
 register i;
 setup("combat");
 if (attach(COMBAT_PORT) != 0)
  crash("initial - combat program already active!\n");
 randomize(SYSTEM_SEED); /* set random number generator. */
 Text_header = (struct tx_textline *) alloc(sizeof(struct tx_textline));
 Text_header->tx_rlink = Text_header;
 Text_header->tx_flink = Text_header;
 for (i=0; i<MAX_PORTS; i++)
  genuser[i].gn_ttynum = -1; /* initialize ttynums to -1 */
 do_initialize();
 errlog("Task initialized.\n");
}
do_loginmsg(loginmsg, tid)
struct io_loginmsg *loginmsg;
unsigned   tid;
{
    register i;
 struct io_logoutmsg logoutmsg;
 struct gn_genuser  *gnptr;
 char *name;
 char buffer[BUFLEN];
 
 for (i=0; i<MAX_PORTS && genuser[i].gn_userindex != 0; ++i)
  ;
 if (i >= MAX_PORTS) { /* task full */
  logoutmsg.io_checkword = IO_CHECKWORD;
  logoutmsg.io_msgnum    = IO_LOGOUTMSG;
  logoutmsg.io_ttynum    = loginmsg->io_ttynum;
  logoutmsg.io_fullerr   = TRUE;
  write_msg(tid, &logoutmsg, sizeof(logoutmsg));
  return(0);
 }
 gnptr = &genuser[i];
 My_port = i;  /* set global masks and port vars */
 user = &user_array[i];
 My_mask = 1 << My_port;
 All_mask |= My_mask;
 if (loginmsg->io_useracct == SUPER_ACCT)
  Staff_mask |= My_mask;
 Allbutme_mask = All_mask & ~My_mask;
 strcpy(gnptr->gn_name, "<login>");
 gnptr->gn_ttynum = loginmsg->io_ttynum;
 gnptr->gn_usernum = loginmsg->io_usernum;
 gnptr->gn_userindex = loginmsg->io_userindex;
 gnptr->gn_useracct  = loginmsg->io_useracct;
 gnptr->gn_tid  = loginmsg->io_tid;
 gnptr->gn_waitmask = 0;
 mwrite(My_mask, "Space Combat Ver 1.1  (small ship)\n");
 mwrite(My_mask,"\nYou climb into the cockpit of your GMB-01X.\n");
 strcpy(buffer, get_tty_login_name(gnptr->gn_ttynum));
 mwrite(My_mask, "Please sign your name in the flight log (%s)",
  buffer);
 user->us_entry = US_ENTERNAME;
 write_text(My_port);
}
int do_restartmsg(restartmsg)
struct io_restartmsg *restartmsg;
{
 if ((My_port = get_port(restartmsg->io_ttynum)) < 0)
  return(My_port); /* if port not active */
 My_mask = 1 << My_port;
 Allbutme_mask = All_mask & ~My_mask;
 write_text(My_port); /* find and send text, adding restart if needed */
 return(My_port);
}
int do_textmsg(textmsg)
struct io_textmsg  *textmsg;
{
 char *text;
 register i;
 if ((My_port = get_port(textmsg->io_ttynum)) <0)
  return(My_port); /* if port not active */
 My_mask = 1 << My_port;
 Allbutme_mask = All_mask & ~My_mask;
 user = &user_array[My_port];
 text = &textmsg->io_start; /* get text line */ 
 if (genuser[My_port].gn_logout) { /* if logout requested */
  write_text(My_port); /* write_text will process logout */
  return(My_port);
 }
 do_input(text);
 write_text(My_port); /* find and send text, adding restart if needed */
 return(My_port);
}
    
do_logout(port)
int  port;
{
 unsigned mask;
 int   i;
 struct  gn_genuser  *gnptr;
 gnptr = &genuser[port];
 mask = 1 << port;
 gnptr->gn_ttynum = -1;
 gnptr->gn_usernum = 0;
 gnptr->gn_userindex = 0;
 gnptr->gn_useracct = 0;
 gnptr->gn_logout = FALSE;
 gnptr->gn_tid = 0;
 gnptr->gn_waitmask = 0;
 All_mask &= ~mask;
 Staff_mask &= ~mask;
}
check_hangup()
{
 register  i;
 unsigned_word index;
 struct gn_genuser *gnptr;
 for (i=0; i < MAX_PORTS; i++) {
  gnptr = &genuser[i];
  if (gnptr->gn_userindex != 0)
   if (get_tty_pid(gnptr->gn_ttynum) != gnptr->gn_tid) {
/* user hung up the phone, or /sys/io task died. */
    All_mask &= ~(1 << i);
    Allbutme_mask = All_mask;
    erasetext(i);
    My_mask = 0;
    user = &user_array[i];
    My_port = i;
    do_hangup();
    do_logout(i);
   }
  
 }
}
;
 All_mask &= ~mask;
 Staff_mask &= ~mask;
}

/*  combat - major combat routines.
  84/05/06. A. E. Klietz
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#include "shipdef.h"
struct  us_user  *user, user_array[MAX_PORTS];
struct  sh_ship  ship[MAX_PORTS];
word  My_ship;
do_initialize()
{
 extern TI_TIME lastupdate;
 time(&lastupdate); /* initialize last update time. */
 day_init();
}
do_hangup()
{
 struct sh_ship *shptr; 
 My_ship = My_port;
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 if (!shptr->sh_exist)
  return;
 else {
  daymsg(My_ship, "HUP", -1);
  mwrite(Allbutme_mask, "### %s (%d) just hung up the phone.\n",
   genuser[My_port].gn_name,  My_ship);
  if (shptr->sh_lasthit != -1) { /* if hit before */
   struct sh_ship *othersh;
   othersh = &ship[shptr->sh_lasthit];
   if (othersh->sh_exist) { /* if other guy still exists */
    mwrite((1 << shptr->sh_lasthit),
     "You got credit for the kill!\n");
    kill_credit(shptr, othersh);
   }
  }
 }
 zero(shptr, sizeof(struct sh_ship));
 user->us_display_range = 60000L;
 user->us_vector = -1;
 shptr->sh_lasthit = -1;
}
    
    
do_input(text)
char *text;
{
 struct  sh_ship  *shptr;
 float  heading, range;
 char  buffer[BUFLEN], buf2[BUFLEN];
 word  i;
 
 My_ship = My_port;
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 if (user->us_entry == US_ENTERNAME) { /* if new user */
  if (text[0] != '\0') { /* if name specified */
   creset_parse();
   cupper_parse(text, text);
   text[11] = '\0';
  }
  else
   strcpy(text, get_tty_login_name(genuser[My_port].gn_ttynum));
  strcpy(buffer, text);
  cstring_tolower(buffer);
  for (i=0; i < MAX_PORTS; ++i) {
   strcpy(buf2, genuser[i].gn_name);
   cstring_tolower(buf2);
   if (equal(buf2, buffer))
    break;
  }
  if (i < MAX_PORTS) { /* if duplicate name */
   mwrite(My_port,"Duplicate name, choose another");
   return;
  }
   
  text[0] = toupper(text[0]);
  strcpy(genuser[My_port].gn_name, text);
  mwrite(All_mask,"### %s (%03u-%04d) just appeared on ship #%d.\n",
   genuser[My_port].gn_name, (unsigned) genuser[My_port].gn_useracct,
   genuser[My_port].gn_userindex, My_ship);
  zero(shptr, sizeof(struct sh_ship));
  range = rnd() * 30000.0 + 10000.0;
  heading = rnd() * 2 * dpie();
  shptr->sh_disp_i = range * cos(heading);
  shptr->sh_disp_j = range * sin(heading);
  heading = rnd() * 2 * dpie();
  shptr->sh_bow_i = cos(heading);
  shptr->sh_bow_j = sin(heading);
  shptr->sh_exist = TRUE;
  shptr->sh_power = 35000L;
{       /* check for enemy ships within 30000km and turn on shields */
  register i;
  
  for (i=0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i)
   if (ship[i].sh_exist && i != My_ship) {
    float i_dist, j_dist;
    i_dist = shptr->sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i;
    j_dist = shptr->sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j;
    if (mag(i_dist, j_dist) < 30000.0) { /* if too close */
     shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_pwr = 25; /* shields up! */
     shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_pwr = 25; 
     break;
    }
   }
}
  shptr->sh_ship_repair = 33;
  shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_repair = 33;
  shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_repair = 33;
  shptr->sh_missles = 25;
  shptr->sh_lasthit = -1;
  user->us_d

show_display()
{
 register i;
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 float  i_vec, j_vec, m;
 boolean  flag = FALSE;
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "Sp Dmg S1 S2 Speed Course Azmuth  Range Heading\n");
 if (user->us_vector >= 0) /* if vectored */
  if (ship[user->us_vector].sh_exist) {
   i_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_i - ship[user->us_vector].sh_disp_i;
   j_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_j - ship[user->us_vector].sh_disp_j;
   show_line(user->us_vector, mag(i_vec, j_vec));
   show_line(My_ship, 0);
  }
  else
   user->us_vector = -1;  /* disable vector */
 if (user->us_vector < 0)
  for (i = 0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i) {
   if (ship[i].sh_exist) {
    i_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i;
    j_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j;
    if ((long) (m = mag(i_vec, j_vec)) <= user->us_display_range ||
      fabs(azmuth(i, My_ship)) < 0.0872664 ||
      fabs(azmuth(My_ship, i)) < 0.0872664)
     show_line(i, m);
    else 
     mwrite(My_mask,"%2d\n",i);
   }    
  }
 else {        
  for (i = 0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i)
   if (ship[i].sh_exist && i != My_ship && i != user->us_vector) 
    if (fabs(azmuth(i, My_ship)) < 0.0872664) {
     mwrite(My_mask,"(%d/", i);
     mwritef(My_mask,"%-6.1f ", azmuth(i, My_ship)
      * 180 / dpie());
     flag = TRUE;
    }
  if (flag)
   mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
 }
  
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "Energy Dmg1 Dmg2 T1/M1/T2 T3 Acel/Tim  Deg/Tim Heat\n");
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6ld %3d%% %3d%% ", shptr->sh_power,
  (int) shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg,
  (int) shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%2d/%2d/%-2d %2d ", shptr->sh_missle1_wait,
  shptr->sh_missles, shptr->sh_missle2_wait,
  shptr->sh_laser_wait);
 mwritef(My_mask,"%4.1f/", shptr->sh_thrust);
 mwrite(My_mask,"%-3d %4d/%-3d %4d\n", 
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft,
  (int) (shptr->s
/*   update - periodic combat update
   84/05/08. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#include "shipdef.h"
TI_TIME  lastupdate;  /* time of last update, in seconds. */
static  word t;
update()
/* Set all vectors, rotations, accelerations, and heat up to date. */
{
 word i;
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 TI_TIME  bindate;
 word  interval;
 boolean  rotflag;
 time(&bindate);
 if ((interval = secdiff(&lastupdate, &bindate)) <= 0) /* if bad time */
  return;
 time(&lastupdate); 
 for (i=0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i)
  if (ship[i].sh_exist) {
   shptr = &ship[i];
   t = interval;
{    /* reset azmuths to out-of-date */
   register j;
  
   for (j=0; j < MAX_SHIPS; ++j)
    shptr->sh_good_azmuth[j] = FALSE;
}                    
   update_misc(i);  /* update miscellaneous things */
   rotflag = FALSE;
   if (shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft > 0 &&
     shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft > 0) {
    rot_accel(i);  /* rotate and accelerate */
    rotflag = TRUE;
   }
   
   if (t > 0 && shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft > 0) {
    rot(i);  /* rotate */
    rotflag = TRUE;
   }
   /* any correction of bow vectors should go here! */
   if (t > 0 && shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft > 0)
    accel(i); /* accelerate */
   drift(i);
  }
}
rot_accel(i)
/* Rotate and accelerate */
word i;
{
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
    word  t1;
 double  r_times_t, a_div_r, a_div_rr, cos_r_times_t;
 double  sin_r_times_t, xn, yn;
 shptr = &ship[i];
 t1 = min(shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft, t);
 t1 = min(shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft, t1); 
 r_times_t = shptr->sh_rotate * t1;
 a_div_rr = (a_div_r = shptr->sh_thrust / shptr->sh_rotate) / shptr->sh_rotate;
 cos_r_times_t = 1.0 - cos(r_times_t);
 sin_r_times_t = sin(r_times_t);
 
 /* compute new displacement (rot and accel) */
 xn = a_div_rr * cos_r_times_t;
 yn = a_div_rr * (r_times_t - sin_r_times_t);
 shptr->sh_disp_i += shptr->sh_vel_i * t1 + 
  shptr->sh_bow_i * xn - shptr->sh_bow_j * yn;
 shptr->sh_disp_j += shptr->sh_vel_j * t1 +
  shptr->sh_bow_j * xn + shptr->sh_bow_i * yn;
 /* compute new velocity (rot and accel) */
 xn = a_div_r * sin_r_times_t;
 yn = a_div_r * cos_r_times_t;
 
 shptr->sh_vel_i += shptr->sh_bow_i * xn - shptr->sh_bow_j * yn;
 shptr->sh_vel_j += shptr->sh_bow_j * xn + shptr->sh_bow_i * yn;
 /* compute new bow position */
 cos_r_times_t = cos(r_times_t);
 xn = cos_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_i -
  sin_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_j;
 shptr->sh_bow_j = sin_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_i +
  cos_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_j;
 shptr->sh_bow_i = xn;
 /* update time variables */
 t -= t1;
 if ((shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft -= t1) <= 0)  { /* if stopped accelerating */
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = 0;
  shptr->sh_thrust = 0.0;
 }
 if ((shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft -= t1) <= 0) { /* if stopped rotating */
  shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft = 0;
  shptr->sh_rotate = 0.0;
 }
}
rot(i)
/* Rotate */
word i;
{
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
    word  t1;
 double  r_times_t, sin_r_times_t, cos_r_times_t;
 double  xn;
 shptr = &ship[i];
 t1 = min(shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft, t);
 sin_r_times_t = sin(r_times_t = shptr->sh_rotate * t1);
 xn = (cos_r_times_t = cos(r_times_t)) * shptr->sh_bow_i
  - sin_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_j;
 shptr->sh_bow_j = sin_r_times_t * shptr->sh_bow_i + cos_r_times_t
  * shptr->sh_bow_j;
 
 shptr->sh_bow_i = xn;
 t -= t1; 
 if ((shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft -= t1) <= 0) { /* if stopped rotating */
  shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft = 0;
  shptr->sh_rotate = 0.0;
 }
}
accel(i)
/* accelerate */
word i;
{
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
    word  t1;
 shptr = &ship[i];
 t1 = min(shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft, t);
 shptr->sh_disp_i += shptr->sh_vel_i * t1 +
  shptr->sh_thrust * shptr->sh_bow_i * t1 * t1 / 2.0;
 shptr->sh_disp_j += shptr->sh_vel_j * t1 +
  shptr->sh_thrust * shptr->sh_bow_j * t1 * t1 / 2.0;
 shptr->sh_vel_i += shptr->sh_thrust * shptr->sh_bow_i * t1;
 shptr->sh_vel_j += shptr->sh_thrust * shptr->sh_bow_j * t1;
 t -= t1; 
 if ((shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft -= t1) <= 0) { /* if stopped accelerating */
  shptr->sh_thrust = 0.0;
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = 0;
 }
}
drift(i)
word i;
{
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
    word  t1;
 if (t <= 0) /* if interval completed */
  return;
 shptr = &ship[i];
 t1 = t;
 shptr->sh_disp_i += shptr->sh_vel_i * t1;
 shptr->sh_disp_j += shptr->sh_vel_j * t1;
 t -= t1;
}
update_misc(i)
word i;
{
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 word  reptime;  /* seconds of repair time */
 word  oldheat;
 shptr = &ship[i];
 /* repair shields and ship */
 if ((reptime = t - shptr->sh_repair_wait) > 0) { /* if repairing */
  if (shptr->sh_ship_dmg > 0) {
   shptr->sh_ship_dmg -= 0.015 * reptime * shptr->sh_ship_repair;
   if (shptr->sh_ship_dmg < 0)
    shptr->sh_ship_dmg = 0;
  }
         
  if (shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg > 0) {
   shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg -=
    0.015 * reptime * shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_repair;
   if (shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg < 0)
    shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg = 0;
        }
  if (shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg > 0) {
   shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg -=
    0.015 * reptime * shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_repair;
  if (shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg < 0)
    shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg = 0;
     }
  shptr->sh_repair_wait = 0; 
 }
 else /* wait for repair */
  shptr->sh_repair_wait -= t;
 /* deduct power usage */
 
 shptr->sh_power -= t*(max(0, shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_pwr  
  - (int) shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg / 4) +
  max(0, shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_pwr 
  - (int) shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg / 4))/2 
  + 25 * fabs(shptr->sh_thrust * min(t, shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft))
  + t;
 if (shptr->sh_power <= 0) { /* if no power */
  mwrite((1 << i),"\nYou are out of power.  Abandon ship!\n\n");
  mwrite((All_mask & ~(1 << i)), "### %s (%d) just lost all power.\n",
   genuser[i].gn_name, i);
  daymsg(i, "PWR", -1);
  shptr->sh_exist = FALSE;
  genuser[i].gn_logout = TRUE;
  return;
 }
 
 /* compute heat buildup and check for explosion */
 oldheat = shptr->sh_heat;
 shptr->sh_heat += 35 * fabs(shptr->sh_thrust) 
  * min(t, shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft)
  - t * 19;
 if (shptr->sh_heat < 0)
  shptr->sh_heat = 0;
 if (shptr->sh_heat >= 8000) { /* boom! */
  shptr->sh_ship_dmg = min(99, (int) shptr->sh_ship_dmg + 10);
  shptr->sh_thrust = (8000 - oldheat)/35 * (shptr->sh_thrust < 0 
   ? -1 : 1);
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = 1;
  shptr->sh_heat = 7999;
  shptr->sh_engine_blown = TRUE;
  shptr->sh_engine_wait = 60;
  shptr->sh_repair_wait = 40;
  mwrite((1 << i),"\007Bang, engines blew up!  Impulse power only.\n");
 }
 if (shptr->sh_engine_blo

/*   subs - general combat subroutines 
   84/05/06. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#include "shipdef.h"
word cparseloc; /* location of next character in buffer for parsing. */
#define  DAY_TOP 254
#define  DAY_BOTTOM 255
#define  DAY_START 1
#define  DAY_LIMIT 21
static  word day_top, day_bottom;  /* top and bottom of dayfile */
static FILE *day_fp;
daymsg(i, reason, by)
/* record dayfile entry for ship[i] with "reason" killed by "by".
   (by = -1 if no killer) */
word i;
char *reason;
word by;
{
 char buffer[256];
 if (i < 0 || i >= MAX_SHIPS) {
  errlog("warn:daymsg - bad ship #%d\n",i);
  return;
 }
 if (day_bottom >= DAY_LIMIT)
  day_bottom = DAY_START;
 else
  ++day_bottom;
 
 random_write(day_fp, DAY_BOTTOM, &day_bottom, sizeof(day_bottom));
 if (day_top == day_bottom) {
  if (day_top >= DAY_LIMIT)
   day_top = DAY_START;
  else
   ++day_top;
  random_write(day_fp, DAY_TOP, &day_top, sizeof(day_top));
 }
 if (by < 0)
  tsprintf(buffer, "%2d  %-9s, %11s %s %3d %03u-%04d  %s",
   i, cdate(), ctime(),
   reason, ship[i].sh_kills, (unsigned) genuser[i].gn_useracct,
   genuser[i].gn_userindex, genuser[i].gn_name);
 else
  tsprintf(buffer, "%2d  %-9s, %11s %s%-2d %2d %03u-%04d  %s",
   i, cdate(), ctime(), 
   reason, by, ship[i].sh_kills, (unsigned) genuser[i].gn_useracct,
   genuser[i].gn_userindex, genuser[i].gn_name);
 buffer[79] = '\0';
 random_write(day_fp, day_bottom, buffer, 80);
}
   
show_dayfile()  
{
 char buffer[80];
 word index;
 
 index = day_bottom;
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "\nSp     Date      Time      Sta  Kl Act-Index Commander\n");
 while (index != day_top) {
  if (random_read(day_fp, index, buffer, 80) <= 0) {
   errlog("dayfile:warn - bad index read %d\n",index);
   break;
  }
  mwrite(My_mask,"%s\n", buffer);
  if (index <= DAY_START)
   index = DAY_LIMIT;
  else
   --index;
 }
 mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
}
day_init()
{
 if ((day_fp = random_open("/data/combat/dayfile")) == 0) /* if cannot open */
  if ((day_fp = random_create("/data/combat/dayfile", 4096, 0)) == 0)
   crash("fatal:combat - unable to create dayfile.\n");
  else { /* init dayfile */
   day_top = day_bottom = DAY_START;
   random_write(day_fp, DAY_TOP, &day_top, sizeof(day_top));
   random_write(day_fp, DAY_BOTTOM, &day_bottom, sizeof(day_bottom));
   random_write(day_fp, day_top, "Dayfile initialized.", 80);
  }
    else {
  random_read(day_fp, DAY_TOP, &day_top, sizeof(day_top));
  random_read(day_fp, DAY_BOTTOM, &day_bottom, sizeof(day_bottom));
 }
}
 
   
 
double mag(i, j)
/* Find the magnitude of the given vector pair. */
double  i, j;
{
 return(sqrt(i*i + j*j));
}
double azmuth(we, they)
/* find azmuth of we-ship to they-ship */
word we, they;
{
 struct sh_ship  *weptr, *theyptr;
 double i_vec, j_vec, m, th;
 if (we < 0 || we >= MAX_SHIPS || they < 0 || they >= MAX_SHIPS
   || we == they) {
  errlog("warn:azmuth - bad we/they parameter.\n");
  return(0.0);
 }
 weptr = &ship[we];
 if (weptr->sh_good_azmuth[they]) /* if already computed */
  return(weptr->sh_azmuth[they]);
 theyptr = &ship[they];
 i_vec = theyptr->sh_disp_i - weptr->sh_disp_i;
 j_vec = theyptr->sh_disp_j - weptr->sh_disp_j;
 m = mag(i_vec, j_vec);
 i_vec /= m;
 j_vec /= m;
 if (fabs(m = i_vec * weptr->sh_bow_i + j_vec * weptr->sh_bow_j) >= 1)
  th = 0;
 else {
  th = acos(m);
     if (j_vec * weptr->sh_bow_i < i_vec * weptr->sh_bow_j)
   th = -th;
 }
 /* update azmuth computation */
 weptr->sh_good_azmuth[they] = TRUE;
 weptr->sh_azmuth[they] = th;
 retu

#include "/std/def.h"

struct cm_cmdlist {
 char *cm_command;
 word cm_index;
} cmdlist[] = {
 "accelerate", 1,
 "brief", 6,
 "eject", 7,
 "end", 7,
 "f", 8,
 "fire", 11,
 "g", 5,
 "l", 11,
 "launch", 12,
 "list", 13,
 "m", 12,
 "p", 3,
 "quit", 7,
 "rotate", 14,
 "s", 4,
 "send", 15,
 "set", 2,
 "show", 16,
 "v", 10,
 "z", 9,
 
};
#define  CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cm_cmdlist))
do_command(buffer)
/* Process all commands */
char *buffer;
{
 struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 char wrd[BUFLEN], wrd2[BUFLEN]; 
 word cmdnum, oldparseloc;
 extern word cparseloc;
 double f,g;
 word i,j;
shptr = &ship[My_ship];
creset_parse();
while (oldparseloc = cparseloc, cparse(buffer, wrd) >= 0) {
 cmdnum = binsearch_ptr(wrd, &cmdlist[0].cm_command,  
  sizeof(struct cm_cmdlist), CMDLIST_LEN);
 if (cmdnum >= 0) /* convert to index */
  cmdnum = cmdlist[cmdnum].cm_index;
 switch (cmdnum) {
case -2: mwrite(My_mask,"\"%s\" is not unique.\n", wrd);
   break;
case -1: mwrite(My_mask, "\"%s\" - no such command.\n", wrd);
   break;
case 2: /* set */
  set(buffer);
  break;
case 16: /* show */
  show(buffer);
  break;
case 1: /* accelerate */
 if (dparse(buffer,wrd) <= 0 || (f = atof(wrd)) == 0.0) {
  shptr->sh_thrust = 0.0;
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = 0;
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask,"Acceleration stopped.\n");
  break;
 }
 if ((f = atof(wrd)) < -5)
  f = -5;
 else
  if (f > 5)
   f = 5;
 if (shptr->sh_engine_blown) {
  if (!user->us_brief && (f < -0.5 || f > 0.5))
   mwrite(My_mask,"Impulse power only.  Accelerating at 0.5km/sec/sec\n");
  if (f < -0.5)
   f = -0.5;
  else       
   if (f > 0.5)
    f = 0.5;
 }
 if (dparse(buffer,wrd) <= 0 || (i = atoi(wrd)) == 0)
  i = 1;
 else
  if (i < 0) {
   shptr->sh_thrust = 0.0;
   shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = 0;
   if (!user->us_brief)
    mwrite(My_mask,"Bad acceleration time.\n");
   break;
  }
  else
   if (i > 300) {
    if (!user->us_brief)
     mwrite(My_mask,"WARNING - engines may overheat.\n");
    i = 300;
   }
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "Firing engines.\n");
    shptr->sh_thrust = f;
 shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft = i;
 break;
 
  
case 6: /* brief */
 user->us_brief = !user->us_brief;
 break;
case 4: /* shield */
case 8: /* full */
case 10: /* vector */
case 3: /* repair */
case 9: /* zero */
case 5: /* display */
 cparseloc = oldparseloc;
 set(buffer);
 break;
case 14: /* rotate */
 if (dparse(buffer,wrd) <= 0 || (f = atof(wrd)) == 0.0) {
 shptr->sh_rotate = 0.0;
  shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft = 0;
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Rotation stopped.\n");
  break;
 }
 if ((f = atof(wrd)) <= -360.0)
  f = -359.9;
 else
  if (f >= 360.0)
   f = 359.9;
 
 if (dparse(buffer,wrd) <= 0 || (i = atoi(wrd)) == 0)
  i = 6;
 else
  if (i < 1 || i > 6) {
   if (!user->us_brief)
    mwrite(My_mask, "Bad rotation rate. 0 < r <= 6 degrees/sec.\n"); 
   shptr->sh_rotate = 0.0;
   shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft

  register i;
  boolean  too_close = FALSE;

   if (ship[i].sh_exist && i != My_ship) {
    float i_dist, j_dist;
    i_dist = shptr->sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i;
    j_dist = shptr->sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j;
    if (mag(i_dist, j_dist) < 30000.0) { /* if too close */
     too_close = TRUE;
     break;

   }
  mwrite(My_mask, "\007DESTRUCT ORDER VERIFIED...\n");

kill_credit(dead_ptr, kill_ptr)
/* Give kill_ptr credit for the death of dead_ptr */
struct sh_ship *dead_ptr, *kill_ptr;
{
 kill_ptr->sh_power += dead_ptr->sh_power + 5000L;
 if (kill_ptr->sh_kills % 5 == 4) /* if 5th kill */
  kill_ptr->sh_missles = 25;
 kill_ptr->sh_kills += 1;
 kill_ptr->sh_repair_wait = 0;
}
cisseparator(ch)
/* Procedure isseparator  - return true if ch is one of the
characters " ,?%#" */
word ch;
{
 char *delim = " ,%#";
 register pos, ch2;
 pos = 0;
 while ((ch2 = delim[pos++]) != '\0')
  if (ch == ch2)
   return(1);
 
 return(0);
}
cstring_tolower(string)
/* Procedure string_tolower - convert a string to lowercase. */
char *string;
{
 register pos;
 pos = 0;
 while (string[pos] != '\0') {
  string[pos] = tolower(string[pos]);
        ++pos;
 }
}
creset_parse()
/* Procedure reset_parse - reset the parse counter. */
{
 cparseloc = 0;
}
cupper_parse(buffer, wrd)
/* Procedure parse - extract a single word from 'buffer' to 'word'.
The size of 'word' must equal the size of 'buffer' to avoid over-
flowing the word string.    The procedure returns the length of the
word, zero if end-of-string. Words are not* converted to lowercase. */
char *buffer, *wrd;
{
 word  wordpos;  /* character pointer for 'word'.*/
 register ch;
 if (cparseloc == -1) { /* end of buffer already encountered. */
  wrd[0] = '\0'; /* guarantee end of string. */
  return(0);
 }
 while ((ch = buffer[cparseloc++]) != '\0')
  if (!cisseparator(ch)) /* skip separators. */
   break;
 if (ch == '\0') { /* return zero if no non-seps found. */
  cparseloc = -1;
        wrd[0] = '\0'; /* guarantee end of string. */
  return(0);
 }
 wordpos = 0;
 if (isdigit(ch) || ch == '-' || ch == '.')  /* fill number */
  do {  /* fill word */
   wrd[wordpos++] = ch;
      ch = buffer[cparseloc++];
  } while ((isdigit(ch) || ch == '.') && wordpos < BUFLEN - 1);
    else
  do {  /* fill word */
   wrd[wordpos++] = ch;
      ch = buffer[cparseloc++];
  } while ((ch != '\0') && !cisseparator(ch) &&
   (!isdigit(ch) && ch != '.' && ch != '-') &&
   wordpos < BUFLEN - 1);
 --cparseloc;
 wrd[wordpos] = '\0'; /* add trailing zero to make proper string. */
 if (ch == '\0') /* end of buffer? */
  cparseloc = -1;
 else {
  while ((ch = buffer[cparseloc++]) != '\0')
   if (!cisseparator(ch)) /* skip separators. */
    break;
  --cparseloc;
  if (ch == '\0')
   cparseloc = -1;
 }
 return(wordpos);
}
 
cparse(string, wrd)
/* Parse - parse string into lowercase words.  
This procedure is identical to upper_parse, except it converts words
to lowercase. */
char *string, *wrd;
{
 word len;
 len = cupper_parse(string, wrd);
 cstring_tolower(wrd);  /* convert to lowercase. */
 if (strlen(wrd) == 2)
  if (equal(wrd, "on") ||
   equal(wrd, "at") ||
   equal(wrd, "to") ||
   equal(wrd, "of"))
   return(cparse(string, wrd));
 return(len);
}

/* parse and reset if parameter is not a digit */
char *string, *wrd;
{
 register i,len;
 i = cparseloc;
 len = cparse(string, wrd);
 if (wrd[0] >= 'a' && wrd[0]P

show_line(shipnum, magnitude)
word shipnum;
double magnitude;
{
 register struct sh_ship *shptr;
 float  i_vec, j_vec, th, m;
 shptr = &ship[shipnum];
 mwrite(My_mask, "%2d %2d%% %2d %2d ", shipnum,
  shptr->sh_ship_dmg, shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_pwr -
       shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg / 4,
       shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_pwr -
       shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg / 4);
 m = mag((i_vec = shptr->sh_vel_i), (j_vec = shptr->sh_vel_j));
 mwritef(My_mask, "%5.0f ", m);
 if (m >= .001) {
  i_vec /= m;
  j_vec /= m;
  if ((m = i_vec * shptr->sh_bow_i + j_vec * shptr->sh_bow_j) >= 1.0)
   th = 0.0;
  else {
   th = acos(m);
      if (j_vec * shptr->sh_bow_i < i_vec * shptr->sh_bow_j)
    th = -th;
  }
 }
 else
  th = 0.0;
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6.1f ", th * 180 / dpie());
 if (My_ship == shipnum) { /* if same ship */
  mwrite(My_mask, "\n");
  return;
 }
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6.1f ", azmuth(My_ship, shipnum) * 180 / dpie());
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6ld ", (long) magnitude);
 mwritef(My_mask,"%6.1f\n", azmuth(shipnum, My_ship) * 180 / dpie());
}
shptr->sh_bow_i + j_vec * shptr->sh_bow_j) >= 1.0)
   th = 0.0;
  else {
   th = acos(m);
      if (j_vec * shptr->sh_bow_i < i_vec * shptr->sh_bow_j)
    th = -th;
  }
 }
 else
  th = 0.0;
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6.1f ", th * 180 / dpie());
 if (My_ship == shipnum) { /* if same ship */
  mwrite(My_mask, "\n");
  return;
 }
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6.1f ", azmuth(My_ship, shipnum
i
/*  display - combat displays.
  84/05/06. A. E. Klietz
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#include "shipdef.h"
show_display()
{
 register i;
 register struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 float  i_vec, j_vec, m;
 boolean  flag = FALSE;
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "Sp Dmg S1 S2 Speed Course Azmuth  Range Heading\n");
 if (user->us_vector >= 0) /* if vectored */
  if (ship[user->us_vector].sh_exist) {
   i_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_i - ship[user->us_vector].sh_disp_i;
   j_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_j - ship[user->us_vector].sh_disp_j;
   show_line(user->us_vector, mag(i_vec, j_vec));
   show_line(My_ship, 0);
  }
  else
   user->us_vector = -1;  /* disable vector */
 if (user->us_vector < 0)
  for (i = 0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i) {
   if (ship[i].sh_exist) {
    i_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i;
    j_vec = ship[My_ship].sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j;
    if ((long) (m = mag(i_vec, j_vec)) <= user->us_display_range ||
      fabs(azmuth(i, My_ship)) < 0.0872664 ||
      fabs(azmuth(My_ship, i)) < 0.0872664)
     show_line(i, m);
    else 
     mwrite(My_mask,"%2d",i);
   }    
  }
 else {        
  for (i = 0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i)
   if (ship[i].sh_exist && i != My_ship && i != user->us_vector) 
    if (fabs(azmuth(i, My_ship)) < 0.0872664) {
     mwrite(My_mask,"(%d/", i);
     mwritef(My_mask,"%-6.1f ", azmuth(i, My_ship)
      * 180 / dpie());
     flag = TRUE;
    }
  if (flag)
   mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
 }
  
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask, "Energy Dmg1 Dmg2 T1/M1/T2 T3 Acel/Tim  Deg/Tim Heat\n");
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 mwritef(My_mask, "%6ld %3d%% %3d%% ", shptr->sh_power,
  shptr->sh_shield[0].sh_shield_dmg,
  shptr->sh_shield[1].sh_shield_dmg);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%2d/%2d/%-2d %2d ", shptr->sh_missle1_wait,
  shptr->sh_missles, shptr->sh_missle2_wait,
  shptr->sh_laser_wait);
 mwritef(My_mask,"%4.1f/", shptr->sh_thrust);
 mwrite(My_mask,"%-3d %4d/%-3d %4d\n", 
  shptr->sh_thrust_timeleft,
  (int) (shptr->sh_rotate * shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft * 180 / dpie()),
  shptr->sh_rotate_timeleft,
  shptr->sh_heat);
}

/*  shoot - combat shoot'em up processor.
  84/05/06. A. E. Klietz
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "combatdef.h"
#include "shipdef.h"
laser(buffer)
char *buffer;
/* shoot laser */
{
 struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 char wrd[BUFLEN]; 
 double width;
 word shld, i, beam, sh, dmg1, dmg2;
 longword dmg;
 boolean hit = FALSE;
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 if (dparse(buffer, wrd) <= 0 || (beam = atoi(wrd)) <= 0) {
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Missing laser energy parameter.\n");
  return;
 }
 if (dparse(buffer, wrd) <= 0 || (width = atof(wrd)) < 1.0)
  width = 1.0;
 if (width > 10.0) {
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Max beam width is 10 degrees.\n");
  width = 10.0;
 }
 if (shptr->sh_laser_wait != 0) {
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Lasers need %d more seconds to cool.\n",
    shptr->sh_laser_wait);
  return;
 }
 if (beam > 2000)
  beam = 2000;
 beam = minf(beam, shptr->sh_power);
 shptr->sh_power -= beam;
 shptr->sh_laser_wait = (int) (5 + beam * 0.0275);
 for (i=0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i) 
  if (i != My_ship && ship[i].sh_exist)
   if (azmuth(My_ship, i) <= width * dpie() / 360) { /* if hit */
    sh = (azmuth(i, My_ship) <= 0.523598776) ?
     0 : 1; /* shield 0 or shield 1? */
    dmg = (beam/width) * 12 * (33 -
     min(ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_pwr,(100 -
     (int) ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg) / 4));
    dmg /= 1 + mag(shptr->sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i,
        shptr->sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j);
    if (dmg > 100)
     dmg = 100;
           
    if (dmg <= 0)
     continue;
    dmg1 = min((int) dmg/2, 100 - (int) ship[i].sh_ship_dmg);
    if (user_array[i].us_brief)
     mwrite((1 << i), "%s S%d lsr hit shld %d for %d%%.\n", 
      dmg1 >= 20 ? "**Blam**" : ">>Pwang<<",
      My_ship, sh+1, dmg1);
    else
     mwrite((1 << i), "%s Ship #%d laser hit shield %d for %d%% damage.\n",
      dmg1 >= 20 ? "**Blam**" : ">>Pwang<<",
      My_ship, sh+1, dmg1);
    if (user->us_brief)
     mwrite(My_mask,"Lsr hit shld %d of S%d for %d%%\n",
      sh+1, i, dmg1);
    else
     mwrite(My_mask,"Laser hit shield %d of ship #%d for %d%% damage.\n",
      sh+1, i, dmg1);
    ship[i].sh_lasthit = My_ship;
    ship[i].sh_repair_wait = 40;
    dmg2 = (sh == 0) ? dmg / 2 : dmg;
    
    ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg = min(100,
     (int) ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg + dmg2);
    hit = TRUE;
    if ((ship[i].sh_ship_dmg += dmg1) >= 100)
     killed(i); /* byebye */
   }
  
 if (!hit && !user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask,"You missed!\n");
}
missle(buffer)
char *buffer;
/* launch missle */
{
 struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 char wrd[BUFLEN]; 
 word sh, dmg1, dmg2;
 longword dmg;
 register i;
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 if (dparse(buffer, wrd) <= 0 || (i = atoi(wrd)) < 0 || i >= MAX_SHIPS
  || !ship[i].sh_exist || i == My_ship) {
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Missing target number.\n");
  return;
 }
 if (shptr->sh_missles <= 0) { /* if no missles */
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "You are out of missles!\n");
  return;
 }
  
 if (shptr->sh_missle1_wait != 0 && shptr->sh_missle2_wait != 0) {
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Missle tubes not yet ready.\n");
  dparse(buffer,wrd);
  return;
 }
 --(shptr->sh_missles);
 if (shptr->sh_missle1_wait == 0)
  shptr->sh_missle1_wait = 60;
 else
  shptr->sh_missle2_wait = 60;
 if (azmuth(My_ship, i) <= 0.043633232 &&
  mag(shptr->sh_disp_i - ship[i].sh_disp_i, 
   shptr->sh_disp_j - ship[i].sh_disp_j) <= 20000) { /* if hit */
  sh = (azmuth(i, My_ship) <= 0.523598776) ?
   0 : 1; /* shield 0 or shield 1? */
  dmg = (long) (102 - min(ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_pwr * 4,
   100 - (int) ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg));
  if (dmg > 100)
   dmg = 100;
         
  dmg1 = min((int) dmg/2, 100 - (int) ship[i].sh_ship_dmg);
  if (user_array[i].us_brief)
   mwrite((1 << i), "%s S%d msl hit shld %d for %d%%.\n", 
    dmg1 >= 20 ? "**Boom**" : ">>Pwing<<",
    My_ship, sh+1, dmg1);
  else
   mwrite((1 << i), "%s Ship #%d missle hit shield %d for %d%% damage.\n",
    dmg1 >= 20 ? "**Boom**" : ">>Pwing<<",
    My_ship, sh+1, dmg1);
  if (user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask,"Msl hit shld %d of S%d for %d%%\n",
    sh+1, i, dmg1);
  else
   mwrite(My_mask,"Missle hit shield %d of ship #%d for %d%% damage.\n",
    sh+1, i, dmg1);
  ship[i].sh_lasthit = My_ship;
  ship[i].sh_repair_wait = 40;
  dmg2 = dmg / 2;
  
  ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg = min(100,
   (int) ship[i].sh_shield[sh].sh_shield_dmg + dmg2);
  if ((ship[i].sh_ship_dmg += dmg1) >= 100)
   killed(i); /* byebye */
 }
 else /* missed */
  if (!user->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Missle missed!\n");
}

   84/05/13. A. E. Klietz.

#include "/std/def.h"

#include "shipdef.h"
static struct cm_showlist {

 word cm_index;
} showlist[] = {
 "center", 1,
 "logfile", 4,
 "parallel", 3,
 "who", 2,
};
#define  SHOWLIST_LEN (sizeof(showlist) / sizeof(struct cm_showlist))
show(buffer)
char *buffer;
{
 struct sh_ship  *shptr;
 char wrd[BUFLEN], wrd2[BUFLEN]; 
 word shownum;
 double f,g,th;
 longword l;
 word i,j;
 shptr = &ship[My_ship];
 cparse(buffer, wrd);
 shownum = binsearch_ptr(wrd, &showlist[0].cm_command,  
  sizeof(struct cm_showlist), SHOWLIST_LEN);
 if (shownum >= 0) /* convert to index */
  shownum = showlist[shownum].cm_index;
 switch (shownum) {
case -2: mwrite(My_mask,"\"%s\" is not unique.\n", wrd);
   break;
case -1: mwrite(My_mask, "\"%s\" - no such SHOW parameter.\n", wrd);
  mwrite(My_mask, "\nValid SHOW parameters are:\n");
  for (i=0; i < SHOWLIST_LEN; ++i)
   if (strlen(showlist[i].cm_command) > 1)
    mwrite(My_mask,"%s\n", showlist[i].cm_command);
  mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
   break;
case 1: /* center */
 f = shptr->sh_disp_i;
 g = shptr->sh_disp_j;
 l = mag(f, g);
 if (l <= 0) /* if sitting on center */
  break;
 f /= l;
 g /= l;
 if (fabs(th = f * shptr->sh_bow_i + g * shptr->sh_bow_j) >= 1)
  th = 0;
 else {
  th = acos(th);
     if (g * shptr->sh_bow_i < f * shptr->sh_bow_j)
   th = -th;
 }
 mwritef(My_mask,"Range %ld km,", l);
 mwritef(My_mask," Azmuth %6.1f to center.\n", th * 180 / dpie());
 if (genuser[My_port].gn_useracct == SUPER_ACCT) { /* if staff */
  mwritef(My_mask,"i-vector = %ld,  ", (long) shptr->sh_disp_i);
  mwritef(My_mask,"j-vector = %ld.\n", (long) shptr->sh_disp_j);
 }
 break;
case 2: /* who */
 mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
 if (!user->us_brief)
  mwrite(My_mask,"Shp Commander   Act-Index Kills\n");
  
 for (i=0; i < MAX_SHIPS; ++i) 
  if (ship[i].sh_exist) {
   mwrite(My_mask,"%2d  %-11s %03u-%04d   ", 
       i, genuser[i].gn_name,
    (unsigned) genuser[i].gn_useracct,
    genuser[i].gn_userindex);
   mwrite(My_mask,"%d\n",ship[i].sh_kills);
  }
 mwrite(My_mask,"\n");
 break; 
case 3: /* parallel */
 if (dparse(buffer, wrd) <= 0 || (i = atoi(wrd)) < 0 || i >= MAX_SHIPS
  || !ship[i].sh_exist || i == My_ship) {

   mwrite(My_mask, "Missing or bad ship number.\n");
  break;

 l = mag(f, g);
 if (l < .00001) {
  mwrite(My_mask,"(Already parallel)\n\n");
  break;
 }
 f /= l;
 g /= l;
 if (fabs(th = f * shptr->sh_bow_i + g * shptr->sh_bow_j) >= 1)
  th = 0;
 else {
  th = acos(th);
     if (g * shptr->sh_bow_i < f * shptr->sh_bow_j)
   th = -th;
 }
 mwrite(My_mask,"%2d ",i);
 mwritef(My_mask,"%6.1f ",th * 180 / dpie());
 mwritef(My_mask, "%5ld ",l);
 th = (th <= 0) ? th + dpie() : th - dpie();
 mwritef(My_mask,"%6.1f ",th * 180 / dpie());
 mwritef(My_mask,"%5ld\n\n", -l);
 break;
case 4: /* logfile */
 show_dayfile();
 break;
 
default:
 mwrite(My_mask, "%s - parameter not implemented yet.\n", wrd);
 break;
  }
}

variables user_data_ptr and system_data_ptr.   It also sets the

   return(0); /* dont change tty settings */

   buffer.tty_RUBOUT = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_backspace;

/*  stringsubs - string process subroutines.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
subset(part, full)
/* Procedure subset - returns TRUE if "part" is a substring
of "full". */
char *part, *full;
{
 char ch;
 while ((ch = *part++) == *full++ && ch != '\0')
  ;
 return(ch == '\0'); 
}
binary_wordmatch(word, wordlist, wordlist_len)
/* Procedure binary_wordmatch scans a alphabetized list for
a unique match and returns the hash code for the word from
the list.  A -1 is returned if not unique, and it issues 
an appropriate error message.  A 0 is returned is not found,
and the calling procedure must issue its own message. */
char *word;
struct  cmdlist_type  wordlist[];
int  wordlist_len;
{
int  diff, lower, upper, pos;
 if (word[0] == '\0')
  return(0);
 lower = 0;
 upper = wordlist_len - 1;
 do {
  pos = (lower + upper) / 2;
  diff = strcmp(wordlist[pos].cmd, word);       
  if (diff <= 0)
   lower = pos + 1;
  if (diff >= 0)
   upper = pos - 1;
  } while (lower <= upper);
 if (equal(wordlist[pos].cmd,word))
  return(wordlist[pos].cmdnum);
 if (!subset(word, wordlist[pos].cmd)) {
  pos++;
  if (pos > wordlist_len - 1 || !subset(word, wordlist[pos].cmd))
   return(0);
 }
 
 if (pos < wordlist_len - 1)
  if (subset(word, wordlist[pos + 1].cmd))
   return(-1); /* not unique error. */
 return(wordlist[pos].cmdnum);
}

  if (diff <= 0)
   lower = pos + 1;
  if (diff >= 0)
   upper = pos - 1;

/*  time - System time and date routines.
  83/09/25. A. E. Klietz.
  dateconv(bindate) - converts the binary date array bindate[2]
into a character string of the format:
   "83 Sep 25"
  timeconv(bindate) - converts the binary date array bindate[2]
into a character string of the format:
   "11:34:45 pm"
 
  date() - returns the date in a string as above.
  time() - returns the time in a string as above.
  secdiff(bindate1, bindate2) - returns the difference in
seconds of bindate2 - bindate1.  If > 18 hours, then it returns
65535.
  mindiff(bindate1, bindate2) - returns the difference in
minutes of bindate2 - bindate1.  If > 22 days, then it returns
31680 minutes.
  count_days(bindate) - returns the number of days since Jan 1st,
1980.
*/
#asm "seg 2"  /* force array into segment 2 (constants) */
static char months[13][4] {
 "???", "Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun",
 "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"
};
#asm "seg 2"
static int year_count[16] {
0, 366, 731, 1096, 1461, 1827, 2192, 2557, 2922, 3288, 3653, 4018, 4383, 
4749, 5114, 5479 };
#asm "seg 2"
static int day_count[2][12] {
0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334,
0, 31, 60, 91, 121, 152, 182, 213, 244, 274, 305, 335 };
char *dateconv(d)                                 
unsigned d[]; /* binary date array [2] */
{                            
 static char datestr[12];
 strcpy(datestr,"           "); /* zero out old date. */
 tsprintf(datestr,"%u %s %u", (d[0] >> 12)+80, months[(d[0] >> 8) & 0xf],
  (d[0] >> 1) & 0x3f);
 
 return(datestr);
}
char *timeconv(d)
unsigned d[]; /* binary date array [2] */
{
 unsigned hour;
 static char timestr[12];
 strcpy(timestr,"           "); /* zero out old time. */
 hour = d[1] / 3600;
 tsprintf(timestr, "%2u:%02u:%02u %s", hour == 0 ? 12 : hour,
  (d[1]%3600)/60, d[1]%60, d[0] & 01 ? "pm" : "am");
 return(timestr);
}
char *date()
{
 unsigned bindate[2];
 get_date(bindate);
 return(dateconv(bindate));
}
char *time()
{
 unsigned bindate[2];
 get_date(bindate);
 return(timeconv(bindate));
}
unsigned secdiff(bindate1, bindate2)
/* Procedure secdiff - return difference in seconds bindate2 - bindate1.
If the difference is more than 18.2 hours, then 65535 is returned. */
unsigned bindate1[], bindate2[];
{
 unsigned datediff, secs, secdiff;
 secs = bindate2[1] - bindate1[1];
 if (bindate1[0] == bindate2[0])
  return(secs);
 else {
  datediff = count_days(bindate2) - count_days(bindate1);
        datediff = datediff * 2 + (bindate2[0] & 0x1) - (bindate1[0] & 0x1);
  secdiff = bindate2[1] - bindate1[1];
  secs = 43200 + secdiff;
/* datediff now has the difference in dates in twelve hour intervals. */
  if (datediff > 2 || datediff == 1 && secdiff >= 22336
   || datediff == 2 && secs >= 22336)
        return(65535);
  else /* datediff must be 1 or 2. */
   return(secs + 43200 * (datediff - 1));
  }
}
unsigned mindiff(bindate1, bindate2)
/* Procedure mindiff - return the difference in minutes bindate2 - bindate1.
The difference is truncated; any remaining seconds are discarded.
If the difference is more than 22 days, then 22 days (31680 min) is
returned. */
unsigned bindate1[], bindate2[];
{
 unsigned datediff, min, sec1, sec2;
 
 sec1 = bindate1[1];
 sec2 = bindate2[1];
 min = (sec2 - sec1) / 60;
 if (bindate1[0] == bindate2[0])
  return(min);
 else {
  datediff = count_days(bindate2) - count_days(bindate1);
  datediff = datediff * 2 + (bindate2[0] & 0x1) - (bindate1[0] & 0x1);
  min = (43200 + sec2 - sec1) / 60;
  if (datediff > 44) /* 22 days. */
   return(31680);  /* 22 days max. */
  else
   return(min + (datediff - 1) * 720);
 }
}
       
int count_days(bindate)
/* Procedure day_count - count the number of days since Jan 1st, 1980. */
unsigned bindate[];
{
 int  month, day, year;
 day = (bindate[0] >> 1) & 0x3F;
 month = (bindate[0] >> 8) & 0xF;
 year = bindate[0] >> 12;
 return(year_count[year] + day_count[year % 4 == 0][month] + day);
}
 & 0x1) - (bindate1[0] & 0x1);
  min = (43200 + sec2 - sec1) / 60;
  if (datediff > 44) /* 22 days. */
   return(31680)

/*  ac_msgs.h - Accounti`g message definitions.
  83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 The following is a list of standard message definitions for the
accounting administrator (/sys/accounting).
 This file is dependent on aekdef.h and globdef.h.
*/
#define AC_CHECKWORD   0x6C
#define ACCOUNTING_PORT  22
enum ac_msgnug_type {GLOBMSG, LOGINMSG, LOGOUTMSG, CONSTMSG,
 NEWAPMSG, INTLOCKMSG, READMSG, WRITEMSG, CREDITMSG, ACMAXMSG};
struct ac_generic_reply {   /* generic ac message */
 int   ac_checkword;
 char  ac_buffer[126];
};
struct ac_globmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
}; 
struct ac_globmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 unsigned ac_global_seg_addr;
 struct user_datum *ac_user_data_ptr;  /* pointer to global user data array. */
 struct system_datum *ac_system_data_ptr;  /* pointer to global system data. */
};
struct ac_loginmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
 char  ac_username[12];
 char  ac_userpw[12];
 char  ac_ttyname[8];
};
struct ac_loginmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_login_success;  /* = 0 if bad pw, =1 if ok. =2 if no credits */
};                              /* = 3 if bad login request. 4 if closed, */
        /* = 5 if low on credits. */
struct ac_logoutmsg {
 int   ac_checkword; 
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
};
struct ac_logoutmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_logout_success;  /* = 0 if bad logout req.  = 1 if ok. */
};
struct ac_constmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
};
struct ac_constmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 char  ac_sysnam[24];
 char  ac_sysloc[24];
};
struct ac_newapmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
 byte  ac_apnum;
};
struct ac_newapmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 int   ac_apnum_success; /* = 1 ok, 0 if bad call, */
          /* 2 if out of credits, 3 if low. */
};
struct ac_intlockmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
}; 
struct ac_intlockmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
};
#ifdef va_defined
/* define read/write only if validation records are defined */
struct ac_readmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
 unsigned ac_userindex;
};
struct ac_readmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 int   ac_readmsg_success; /* 0 if bad, userindex if good. */
 int   ac_dummyr;   /* dummy fill */
 struct  va_user_record  ac_user_record;
};
struct ac_writemsg {
 int   ac_checkword;

/*  validef.h - validation file layout.
  83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 This file defines the format of a record in the validation file.
(/val/validuz and /val/valindx)
 This file depends on aekdef.h.

#define   va_defined
struct va_user_record {
 char  va_username[12]; /* codename of user. */
 char  va_hashpw[12];  /* hashed password. */
 unsigned    va_useracct;  /* user account number. (6001-6254) */
 unsigned va_userindex;    /* user index number. (001-999) */
 unsigned  va_ttyoptions;      /* ECHO, TABS, etc. */
 int   va_creditbalance;   /* number of credits remaining. */
            /*  (negative if in debt.) */
 unsigned va_lastlogin[2];    /* date of last login. */
 boolean  va_closed;    /* true if account is closed. */
 unsigned va_birthdate;  /* birthdate of user. */
 byte  va_rating;   /* credit "rating" of user. 0-worst. 10-best. */
 byte  va_backspace;  /* desired backspace char. */
 byte  va_linedel;      /* desired line-delete char. */
 boolean  va_nocharge;  /* true if no charge to this acct. */
 char  va_stringbuf[192]; /* string buffer with 
         name
         1st addr line.
         2nd addr line.
         3rd addr line.
               phone
         hobbies/interests  */
 boolean  va_stateres;  /* true is user lives in state (tax) */
 boolean     va_anonymous;  /* true if user wants to be anonymous */
 byte  va_dummy[19];  /* dummy blank fill. */
};
/* va_user_record must be exactly* 256 characters long. */ 
/* The following is a definition for an index record in the
validation index file.  /config/valindx. It must be 16 bytes long.
*/
struct vi_user_hash {
 char  vi_username[12];    /* last two chars of username are lost. */
 unsigned vi_userindex;
 unsigned    vi_link;     /* pointer to next index entry in hash slot. */
};

 boolean     va_anonymous;  /* true if user wants to be anonymous */

};

/* 
/*  valmsgs - request operations on validation file to accounting.
  83/11/22.  A. E. Klietz.
  interlock_validfile(flag)   TRUE to lock, FALSE to unlock.
  read_valid(userindex, &user_record) read data to user_record
    returns 0 if bad index.
  write_valid(userindex, &user_record) write user_record to file
    returns 0 if bad write.
  str_skip(count, string) skip forward *count* strings in a multiple
    string array.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/validef.h"
#include "/def/ac_msgs.h"
extern char  My_tty;
extern struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
 
interlock_validfile(flag)
/* procedure interlock_validfile - halt the accounting administrator if TRUE,
   restart the administrator if FALSE */
int  flag;
{ 
 static boolean  prevflag;
 struct ac_intlockmsg  intlockmsg;
 struct ac_intlockmsg_reply intlockmsg_reply;
 if (flag == prevflag)
  error("fatal:ilock - lock flag did not change!\n");
 if (!flag) {
  signal(ACCOUNTING_PORT); /* wake up accounting */
  prevflag = flag;
  breakable(); /* restore interrupts */
 }
 else { /* put accounting to sleep */
 unbreakable(); /* disable interrupts */
 intlockmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 intlockmsg.ac_msgnum = INTLOCKMSG;
  
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &intlockmsg, &intlockmsg_reply,
  sizeof(intlockmsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:ilock - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 prevflag = flag;
    }
}
 
read_valid(userindex, user_record)
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;
{ 
 struct ac_readmsg  readmsg;
 struct ac_readmsg_reply readmsg_reply;
 readmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 readmsg.ac_msgnum = READMSG;
 readmsg.ac_ttynum   = My_tty;
 readmsg.ac_userindex = userindex;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &readmsg, &readmsg_reply,
  sizeof(readmsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:vread - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 
 if (readmsg_reply.ac_readmsg_success == 0)
  error("Bad validuz read.\n");
 else
  copy(user_record, &readmsg_reply.ac_user_record, 
   sizeof(struct va_user_record));
}
 
write_valid(userindex, user_record)
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;
{ 
 struct ac_writemsg  writemsg;
 struct ac_writemsg_reply writemsg_reply;
 writemsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 writemsg.ac_msgnum = WRITEMSG;
 writemsg.ac_ttynum  = My_tty;
 writemsg.ac_apnum = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_apnum;
    user_record->va_userindex = userindex;
  
 copy(&writemsg.ac_user_record, user_record,
  sizeof(struct va_user_record));
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &writemsg, &writemsg_reply,
  sizeof(writemsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:vwrite - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 
 if (writemsg_reply.ac_writemsg_success == 0)
  error("Bad validuz write.\n");
}
char *str_skip(count, string)
/* procedure str_skip - skip 'count' strings forward in a list of strings. */
int  count;
char *string;
{
 register   i;
 for (i=1; i<=count; i++) 
  while (*string++ != '\0')
   ;
 return(string);
}

/*  control - GamBit system control program.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
  This system control program handles most system maintenance
and control functions.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include <dev.h>
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/jourdef.h"
#include "/def/appdef.h"
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define EXC_BREAK 2
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, User_num;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
boolean  shutdown; /* TRUE if shutdown in progress. */
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
    "account", 14,  "broadcast", 3,
 "close", 1,  "crash", 2,  "end", 4,  "exit", 4,
 "help", 12,  "idle", 15,     "list", 11, "logoff", 6,"menu", 4,
  "modval",13,  "monitor", 7,  "open", 8,  "quit", 4,
 "shutdown", 10  };
  
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
int  *monflag;  /* pointer to monitored port. */
main()
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
 
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
  
 print(" \n");
 print("Type 'help' for help.\n");
 
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
    if(!setexit()) {
  if (monflag != 0)
   @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_monitoring = FALSE;
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
     for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
        getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
         print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
        }
  else
   print("*INT*\n");
  if (monflag != 0) { /* end monitoring */
   @monflag = 0;
   monflag = 0;
   print("End monitoring.\n");
  }
  if (shutdown) {
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = FALSE;
   shutdown = FALSE;
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = TRUE;
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = FALSE;
   print("\007Shutdown cancelled.  System open.  Logins enabled.\n");
  }
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Enter control command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF)   /* force a hangup command. */
    exit(0);
  } while (len == 0);
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}
docommand(command)
/* Procedure docommand - process menu command. */
char *command;
{
 BUFTYPE word;
 int  cmdnum, i;
 extern reset_parse();
 reset_parse();
 if (parse(command,word) == 0)
  return(0);
 cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
 
 switch (cmdnum) {
 case -1: print("\"%s\" is not unique.\n", word);
    break; /* not unique. */
    case  0: print("\"%s\" is not a command, type 'help' for help.\n",
                word);
    break;
 case  1: /* close */
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = FALSE;
   print("Logins disabled.  System closed.\n");
   break;
 case  2: /* crash */
   print("WARNING! Are you sure you want to do an emergency crash? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word,word);
   if (word[0] == 'y') {
    @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = TRUE;
    kill_all_tasks();
/* Kill all user tasks except ours. */
    print("System crashed.\n");    
    if (Dads_tid == IDLE)
     transform("/cmds/qnxsh", 0, 0);
    else
     exit(0);
   } 
   break;   
 case 3: /* broadcast */
 {
   extern  int  parseloc;
   int  port_num, len;
   FILE *fp;
   char port_name[8];
  
   len = parseloc;
         while (len <= 0) {
    print("Enter message to broadcast? ");
    len = readline(command);
    reset_parse();
   }
   while (isseparator(command[parseloc]))
    parseloc++;  /* skip leading spaces */
   for (port_num = 0; port_num < NUMDEVICES; port_num++) 
    if (@&user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_userindex > 0) {
     strcpy_star_from_at(port_name, user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_ttyname);
     fp = fopen(port_name, "wv");
     tfprintf(fp, "\n\007%s   Note: %s\n\n", time(),
     &command[parseloc]);
     fclose(fp);
    }
   print("Message sent.\n");
   break;
 }
 case 4: /* exit */
   print("Returning to main menu..\n");
   transform("/sys/menu", 1, 0);
   break;
  case 12: /* help */
    dohelp(command, "/help/control");
    break;
 case 11: /* list commands */
   print("The available control commands are:\n");
   {
    int i, skip, index;
    skip = (CMDLIST_LEN / 3) + 1;
    for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
     if (i % 3 == 0)
      print("\n");
     index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
     if (index < CMDLIST_LEN)
      print("%-11s",cmdlist[index].cmd);
    }
   }
   print("\n");
   break;
 case  6: /* logoff */
  {
  int  port_num, tid;
  if (parse(command, word) <= 0) {
   print("Missing tty number.\n");
   return(-1);
  }
  port_num = atoi(word);
  print("Are you sure you want to log off tty%d? ",port_num);
  readline(word);
  reset_parse();
  parse(word, word);
  if (word[0] == 'y') {
   while ((tid = tty_tid(port_num)) != IDLE)
    set_exception(tid, 0x0001, 0); /* force hangup */
   print("tty%d logged off.\n",port_num);
  }
  break;
 }
 case  7: /* monitor */
 {
  int  port_num;
  if (parse(command, word) <= 0) {
   print("Missing tty number.\n");
   return(-1);
  }
  if ((port_num = atoi(word)) < 0 || port_num > NUMDEVICES)
   print("Bad port number.\n");  
  else {
            monflag = &user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_monitoredby;
   if (@monflag != 0) {
    print("Port already monitored elsewhere.\n");
    monflag = 0;
   }
   else {
    BUFTYPE string;
    int  *monitoring;
    unsigned tid;
    @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_monitoredby = 0;
    @monflag = (My_tty == 0) ? -1 : My_tty;
    monitoring = &user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_monitoring;
    @monitoring = TRUE;
    
    print("Monitoring tty%d...\n", port_num);
    for (;;) { /* loop forever */
     if (tty_tid(port_num) == -1 || @monflag == 0 || @monitoring == FALSE ||
        (tid = receive(0, string, BUFLEN)) == 0) {
      @monflag = 0;
      monflag = 0;
      @monitoring = FALSE;
      print("\007tty disconnected.\n");
      break;
     }
     reply(tid, 0, 0);
     string[BUFLEN - 1] = '\0';
     print("%s",string);
    }
   }
  }
  break;
 }
 case  8: /* open */
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = FALSE;
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = TRUE;
   print("Logins enabled.  System open.\n");
   break;
 case 10: /* shutdown */
 {
   boolean  bootflag;
   if (@&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash) {
    print("System shutdown already in progress elsewhere.\n");
    break;
   }
   parse(command,word);
   bootflag = (word[0] != '\0') && subset(word, "boot");
   print("Are you sure you want to shut down the system? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word, word);
   if (word[0] == 'y') {
    int  minutes, i;
    
    print("How many minutes until shutdown? ");
    readline(word);
    reset_parse();
    minutes = atoi(word);
    @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = TRUE;
    shutdown = TRUE;
    do {
     for (i=0; i <= NUMDEVICES; i++)
      if (@&user_data_ptr[i].gl_userindex > 0) {
       FILE *fp;
       strcpy_star_from_at(word, user_data_ptr[i].gl_ttyname);
       fp = fopen(word, "wv");
       tfprintf(fp, "\n%s. \007System shutdown in %d minutes.\n",
        time(), minutes);
       if (minutes <= 5)
        tfprintf(fp, "Logins disabled, please log out.\n");
       fclose(fp);
      }
     if (minutes <= 5)
      @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = FALSE;
     minutes = minutes / 2;
     sleep(minutes * 60);
    } while (minutes > 0);     
    sleep(60);
                @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = TRUE;
    kill_all_tasks();
    sleep(10);
    print("System shutdown complete.\n");       
    if (bootflag) {
     asm(" abs jmp  0,0ffffh");  /* boot system */
    }
    if (Dads_tid == IDLE)
     transform("/cmds/qnxsh", 0, 0);
    else
     exit(0);
   }
 }
            break;
 case 13:
   transform("/cmds/modval", 0, 0);
      break;
    case 14: /* account file */
   parse(command, word);
   if ((i = atoi(word)) == 0 && (i = valid_hash(word)) == 0) {
    print("Bad or missing username parameter.\n");
    return(-1);
   } else {
    char  name[20];
    struct jr_journal_record journal_record;
    FILE  *fp;
    extern char *timeconv(), *dateconv();
 
    tsprintf(name, "/jou/%04u", i);
    if ((fp = fopen(name, "rv")) == 0) {
     print("File missing or busy.\n");
     return(-1);
p
