/*  menu - GamBit menu program.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
  'menu' is a program that handles all introductory communication
with the user.
  If argc > 1 then skip the sysnote and other login checks.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/validef.h"
#include "/def/appdef.h"
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define EXC_BREAK 2
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, User_num;
extern int  Error_status;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
#asm "seg 2" /* force into constant data segment */
static char  cmdnames[][12] = {
 "bye        ",
 "clock      ",
 "control    ",
 "credit     ",
 "diplomacy  ",
 "goodbye    ",
 "help       ",
 "list       ",
 "mail       ",
 "news       ",
 "password   ",
 "scepter    ",
 "send       ",
 "set_option ",
 "talk       ",
 "tasks      ",
 "users      ",
 "who        "
};
#asm "seg 2"   /* force into constant data segment */
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
 0, 1,
 0, 15,
 0, 13,
 0, 12,
 0, 2,
 0, 1,
 0, 3,
 0, 11,
 0, 4,
 0, 5,
 0, 16,
 0, 6,
 0, 17,
 0, 14,
 0, 7,
 0, 8,
 0, 9,
 0, 10
};
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
main(argc, argv)
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
boolean first_cmd = TRUE;
 
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
 
 if (argc <= 1) { /* if initial call to menu. */
  if (Dads_tid == IDLE) /* dont change console's app */
   @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_apnum = AP_MENU;
  print("                _\n");
  print("Welcome to the GamBit Menu.\n");
  if ((fp = fopen("/sys/note","r")) != 0) { /* show sysnote. */
   while (freadline(fp,buffer) != EOF)
    print("%s\n",buffer);
         fclose(fp);
  }
  
  if (!(@&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen))
   if ((unsigned) User_num != 255) {
    print("System closed, sorry.\n");
    exit(0);
   }
   else
    print("(System closed for non-staff.)\n");
  print(" \n");
  print("Type 'help' for help.\n");
 } 
 else
  if (Dads_tid == IDLE) /* console does not issue sendap's */
   sendap(AP_MENU);
 for (len=0; len < CMDLIST_LEN; len++) /* set pointers in cmdlist */
  cmdlist[len].cmd = cmdnames[len];
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
     if(!setexit()) {
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
      for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
         getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
        print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
      }
   else
      print("*INT*\n");
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 if (first_cmd) { /* list commands only on first iteration of cmd loop */
  first_cmd = FALSE;
  print("\n");
  list_cmds();
  print("\nType 'list' to list these commands.\n");
 }
  
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Enter menu command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF) {  /* force a hangup command. */
    strcpy(buffer,"bye");
    len = 3;
   }
  } while (len == 0);
  if ((User_num == -1) && equal(buffer, "manager")) {
   if (Dads_tid == IDLE) 
    sendap(AP_SYSTEM); /* switch tables + journal entry */
   transform("/cmds/qnxsh", 0, 0);
  }
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}
docommand(command)
/* Procedure docommand - process menu command. */
char *command;
{
 BUFTYPE word;
 int  cmdnum, i;
 extern reset_parse();
 reset_parse();
 if (parse(command,word) == 0)
  return(0);
 cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
 
 switch (cmdnum) {
 case -1: print("\"%s\" is not unique.\n",word);
    break;  /* not unique. */
    case  0: print("\"%s\" is not a command, type 'help' for help.\n",
                word);
    break;
 case  1: /* bye */
  { int credit_left;
       credit_left = credit_update(My_tty, -1); /* round result */
    print("Goodbye, come again!\n");
    print("Logoff at %s, on %s.\n",time(),date());
    print("%d credits used.  Account balance= %d cr.\n",
    @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_startcredits - credit_left,
    credit_left);
    exit(0);
  }
    break;
 case  2:
 case  6:
    print("Sorry, that feature hasn't been implemented yet.\n");
    break;
 case  3: /* help */
    dohelp(command, "/help/help");
    break;
 case  4: /* mail */
   sendap(AP_MAIL);
   transform("/sys/mail", 0, 0);
   sendap(AP_MENU);
   break;
 case  5: /* news */
    {
     FILE *fp;
    if ((fp = fopen("/sys/news","r")) != 0) { 
     BUFTYPE buffer;
     while(freadline(fp,buffer) != 0)
      print("%s\n",buffer);
     fclose(fp);
    }
    else
     print("There is no news today.\n");
    };
    break;
 case 7:  /* talk */
   sendap(AP_TALK);
   transform("/sys/io", "21", "gtalk");
   sendap(AP_MENU);
   break;
 case  8: /* task */
   {
    BUFTYPE  word2;
                        
    if (parse(command,word2) > 0)
     if (subset(word2,"size"))
      call("/cmds/task","+s",0);
     else
      print("Unknown task option.\n");
    else
     call("/cmds/task", 0, 0);
   };
   break;
 case 10: /* who */
   if (parse(command,word) <= 0)
    call("/cmds/whog",0,0);
   else {
    int i;
    unsigned index = 0;
    BUFTYPE  name;
    
    if (subset(word, "console")) {
     print("console - system console (not staffed)\n");
     return(0);
    }
    for (i=0; i<NUMDEVICES && index == 0; i++) {
     strcpy_star_from_at(name, user_data_ptr[i].gl_username);    
     string_tolower(name);
     if (subset(name, word))        
      index = @&user_data_ptr[i].gl_userindex;
    }
    if (index == 0 && (index = valid_hash(word)) == 0)
      print("Name not found.\n");
    else
     user_display(index);
   }
   break;
 case 11: /* list commands */
   list_cmds();
   break;
 
 case 12: /* credits left */
   {
    int credit_left;
    credit_left = credit_update(My_tty, 0);
    print("You have used %d credits since login,\n",
     @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_startcredits - credit_left);
    print("and have %d credits remaining in\n",credit_left);
    print("your account.\n");
   }
   break;
 case 13: /* control system */
   if (User_num != -1)
    print("Command restricted, sorry.\n");
   else
    transform("/cmds/control", 0, 0);
   break;
 case 14: /* set command */
{
 struct gl_user_datum *user_ptr;
   user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[My_tty];
   if (User_num == 1) { /* guest account */
    print("Sorry, the guest account cannot change its terminal parameters.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
   while (parse(command,word) <= 0) {
    print("Select one of these options:\n");
    print("     backspace\n");
    print("     duplex\n");
    print("     echo_backspace\n");    
    print("     line_erase\n");    
    print("\nEnter set option? ");
          reset_parse();
    if (readline(command) <= 0)
     return(0);
   }
   if (subset(word, "backspace")) {
    print("Do you want to use\n");
    print("     1) backspace key (Control-H)\n");
    print("     2) delete key    (DEL)\n");
    print("for erasing a character?\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else
     @&user_ptr->gl_backspace =
      (i == 1) ? 0x08 : 0x7F;   
   } else if (subset(word, "duplex")) {   
    print("Do you want to set\n");
    print("     1) full duplex\n");
    print("     2) half duplex\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2)  {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions &= ~ECHO;
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions |= (i == 1 ? ECHO : 0);
    }
   } else if (subset(word, "echo_backspace")) {
    print("Do you want\n");
    print("     1) Backspaces echoed with space/back/space\n");
    print("     2) No special echoing of backspaces\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions &= ~EDEL;
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions |= (i == 1 ? EDEL : 0);
    }
   } else if (subset(word, "line_erase")) {
    print("Select the character you wish to use for erasing a line:\n");
    print("     1) Control-U  (NAK)\n");
    print("     2) Control-X  (CAN)\n");
    print("     3) Escape key (ESC)\n");
    print("\nSelect 1, 2, or 3 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 3) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     switch (i) {
  case 1: i = 0x15;
    break;
  case 2: i = 0x18;
    break;
  case 3: i = 0x1B;
    break;
  }
     @&user_ptr->gl_linedel = i;
    }
   } else {
    print("No such option.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
         set_glob();  /* set up new parameters */
   print("Option set to new value.\n");
   break;
}
    
 case 15:  /* clock */
   print("%s, %s.  %s time.\n", time(), date(),
    (@&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? "Peak" : "Non-peak"));
   { unsigned bindate[2];
    bindate[0] = @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[0];
    bindate[1] = @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[1];
    print("System up at %s, %s.\n", timeconv(bindate),
     dateconv(bindate));
   }
   break;
 case 16: /* change_pw */
   if (User_num == 1) {
    print("Sorry, the guest account cannot change its password.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
   print("Enter old password? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word,word);
   hashpassword(word, word);
   {
    struct va_user_record user_record;
    
    if (read_valid(@&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_userindex,
     &user_record) == 0) 
     print("Bad validuz read.\n");
    else 
     if (!equal(user_record.va_hashpw, word))
      print("Improper password.\n");
     else {
      BUFTYPE word2;
      print("Enter new password? ");
      readline(word);
      print("Enter new password again? ");
      readline(word2);
                        if (!equal(word, word2))
       print("Passwords didn't match.\n");
      else {
       reset_parse();
       parse(word, word);
       word[11] = '\0';
       if (strlen(word) < 6)
        print("Passwords must be at least 6 characters long.\n");
       else {
        hashpassword(word, user_record.va_hashpw);
           if (write_valid(user_record.va_userindex,
          &user_record) == 0)     
         print("Bad validuz write.\n");     
        else        
         print("Password changed.\n");         
       }
      }
     }
   }
            break;
 case 9: /* users */
   print("\nList of all users:\n");
   {
    FILE *fp;
    int  i = 0;
    char name[16];
    fp = fopen("/val/valindx", "rv");
    
    fiseek(fp, 512);  /* skip hash table */
    while(fget(name, 16, fp) > 0) {
     print("%-11s ",name);
     if (++i % 3 == 0)
      print("\n");
    }
    print("\n");
    fclose(fp);
   }
   break;
 case 17: /* send msg to user */
 {
   extern  int  parseloc;
   int  len, ttynum = -1;
   FILE *fp;
   char name[12], tty_name[8];
   
   while (parse(command, word) <= 0) {
    print("Send message to whom? ");
    readline(command); 
    reset_parse();
   }
   len = (parseloc <= 0 ? 0 : 1);
   while (len == 0) { /* end of line */
    print("Enter message to send? ");
    len = readline(command);
    reset_parse();
   }
   while (isseparator(command[parseloc]))
    parseloc++;  /* skip leading spaces */
   for (i=0; i<NUMDEVICES && ttynum == -1; i++) {
    strcpy_star_from_at(name, user_data_ptr[i].gl_username);    
    string_tolower(name);
    if (subset(word, name))        
     ttynum = i;
   }
   if (ttynum < 0) {
    print("No such user logged in.\n"); 
    return(-1);
    }
   strcpy_star_from_at(tty_name, user_data_ptr[ttynum].gl_ttyname);
   fp = fopen(tty_name, "wv");
   strcpy_star_from_at(word, user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_username);
   tfprintf(fp, "\n\007Message from %s:  \"%s\"\n", word,
     &command[parseloc]);
   fclose(fp);
   print("Message sent to %s.\n", name);
   break;
 }
 default:
   print("warn:menu - no such command index!\n");
   break;
 }
}
list_cmds()
/* list available commands */
{
  int  i, skip, index;
  print("The available menu commands are:\n");
  {
   skip = (CMDLIST_LEN / 3) + 1;
   for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
    if (i % 3 == 0)
     print("\n");
    index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
    if (index < CMDLIST_LEN)
     print("%-11s",cmdlist[index].cmd);
   }
  }
  print("\n");
}
user_display(index)
/* procedure user_display - display some information about user */
unsigned index;
{
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 BUFTYPE word;
 extern char *str_skip();
 int  i;
 if (read_valid(index, &user_record) != 0) {
  user_record.va_username[0] = toupper(user_record.va_username[0]);
  user_record.va_stringbuf[0] = toupper(user_record.va_stringbuf[0]);
  
  print("%s's real name is %s.  %s's interests are\n",
   user_record.va_username, (user_record.va_anonymous ?
   "Anonymous" : user_record.va_stringbuf),
   user_record.va_username);
  print("%s\n", str_skip(5, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  reset_parse();
  strcpy(word, str_skip(3, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  if (word[0] == '\0') {
   reset_parse();
   strcpy(word, str_skip(2, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  }
   for (i=0; word[i] != '\0'; i++)   
    if (atoi(&word[i]) != 0) /* dont copy zip code */
     word[
2
/*  modval - modify validation file.
  83/10/10.  A. E. Klietz.
  This program handles all modifications of the validation file
(/val/validuz) outside of normal accounting.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include <dev.h>
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/validef.h"
#include "/def/ac_msgs.h"
#include "/def/dollar.h"
#define EXC_BREAK 2
extern  byte User_num;
struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
 "create", 1,
 "end", 2,
 "exit", 2,
 "help", 4,
 "list", 5,
 "modify", 6,
 "receive", 7,
 "show", 8
};
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
static struct cmdlist_type modlist[] = {
 "account", 3,
 "anonymous",15,
 "birthdate", 9,
 "charge", 12,
 "codename", 1,
 "control-h", 6,
 "credit", 7,
 "echo", 4,
 "edel", 5,
 "linedel", 11,
 "mailaddr", 13,
 "open", 8,
 "password", 2,
 "rating", 10,
 "state_resident",14
};
#define MODLIST_LEN (sizeof(modlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
main()
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
 if (User_num != -1)
  error("fatal:modval - illegal user access.\n");
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
  
 print(" \n");
 
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
    if(!setexit()) {
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
     for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
        getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
         print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
        }
  else
   print("*INT*\n");
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Validation command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF)   /* force a hangup command. */
    exit(0);
  } while (len == 0);
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}
docommand(command)
/* Procedure docommand - process command. */
char *command;
{
 BUFTYPE word;
 int  cmdnum, i;
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 extern reset_parse();
 reset_parse();
 if (parse(command,word) == 0)
  return(0);
 cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
 
 switch (cmdnum) {
 case -1: print("\"%s\" is not unique.\n",word);
    break;  /* not unique. */
    case  0: print("\"%s\" is not a command, type 'help' for help.\n",
                word);
    break;
    case  1: /* create */
  zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));  /* zero record */    
   user_record.va_ttyoptions = ECHO + EDIT + ETAB + ERS
    + EDEL + XON_XOFF + MAPCR;
   user_record.va_backspace = 0x08;
   user_record.va_linedel = 0x1B;
   user_record.va_stateres = TRUE;
   get_date(&user_record.va_lastlogin);
   for (i=1; i<=MODLIST_LEN; i++)
    while(!inp_data(i, &user_record))
     print("Invalid parameter, reenter.\n");
   print("Adding %s to validation file..\n",user_record.va_username);
   interlock_validfile(TRUE);  /* freeze accounting */
   if ((user_record.va_userindex = i = valid_create(&user_record.va_username)) == 32767)
    print("Duplicate entry.\n");
   else
    if (i == 0)
     print("Unable to create entry.\n");
    else 
     if (valid_write(i, &user_record) == 0)
      print("Bad record write (%d).\n",i);
     else
      print("Entry for %s created.\n",user_record.va_username);
   interlock_validfile(FALSE);  /* restore accounting */
   break;   
 case 2:  /* exit */
   print("Returning to control program..\n");  
   transform("/cmds/control", 0, 0);
   break;
 case 4:  /* help */
    dohelp(command, "/help/modval");
    break;
 case 5:  /* list */
   print("The available validation commands are:\n");
   cmd_display(cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
   print("\nThe available modify parameters are:\n");
   cmd_display(modlist, MODLIST_LEN);
   break;
 case 6:  /* modify */
   parse(command, word);
   if ((i = atoi(word)) == 0 && (i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)
    print("No such username.\n");
   else {
    if (read_valid(i, &user_record) == 0) 
     print("Bad valid read.\n");
    else {
     parse(command, word);
     cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, modlist, MODLIST_LEN);
     if (cmdnum == 1)
      print("Close and recreate account to change name.\n");
     else
      if (inp_data(cmdnum, &user_record) == TRUE) 
       if (write_valid(i, &user_record) == 0)
        print("Bad valid write.\n");
       else
        print("Entry modified.\n");
       }
   }
   break;
    
 case 7: /* receive credits.*/
   parse(command,word);
   if ((i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)
    print("No such user.\n");
   else {
     int  credits;
    parse(command, word);
    if ((credits = atoi(word)) < 1 || credits > 200) {
     if (credits == 0) {
      print("Missing dollar parameter.\n");
      return(-1);
     }
     print("\007 Are you SURE it's %d dollars? ",credits);
     if (getflag() == FALSE)
      return(-1);
    }
     print("Adding %d dollars to account.\n",credits);
     if (abs(credits) >= 4096) {
      print("\007 Credit account overflow!\n");
      return(-1);
     }
     credits *= CREDITSPERDOLLAR;
     send_credits(i, credits);
   }
   break;
 case 8: /* show */
   parse(command,word);
   if ((i = atoi(word)) == 0 && (i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)   
    print("No such user.\n");
            else 
    if (read_valid(i, &user_record) == 0)
     print("Bad valid read.\n");
    else
     display(&user_record);
   break;
 default:
   print("warn:modval - no such command index!\n");
    break;
 }
}
cmd_display(list, list_len)
struct cmdlist_type list[];
int  list_len;
{
 int i, skip, index;
 skip = (list_len / 3) + 1;
 for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
  if (i % 3 == 0)
   print("\n");
  index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
  if (index < list_len)
   print("%-11s",list[index].cmd);
 }
 print("\n");
}
display(user_record)
/* procedure display - display user's data record on output. */
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 char *data;
 unsigned opts;
 char onoff[2][6];
 extern char *str_skip();
 strcpy(onoff[FALSE],"*OFF*");
 strcpy(onoff[TRUE],"*ON*");
    print("\n");
 print("%s, Account: %03d.  Userindex: %04d.  Balance: %d cr.\n",
    user_record->va_username, user_record->va_useracct,
    user_record->va_userindex, user_record->va_creditbalance);
 print("Last login: %s, %s.\n",
    dateconv(user_record->va_lastlogin),
    timeconv(user_record->va_lastlogin));
 print("Bday: %s, CRat: %d.   Account: %s.\n",
    dateconv(&user_record->va_birthdate), user_record->va_rating,
    user_record->va_closed ? "CLOSED" : "Open");
 data = user_record->va_stringbuf;
 print(" Name: %s\n",str_skip(0,data));
 print("Addr1: %s\n",str_skip(1,data));
 print("Addr2: %s\n",str_skip(2,data));
 print("Addr3: %s\n",str_skip(3,data));
 print("Phone: %s\n",str_skip(4,data));
 print("Interests: %s\n",str_skip(5,data));
 print("\n");
 opts = user_record->va_ttyoptions;
 print("    Full-duplex......... %s\n",onoff[(opts & ECHO) != 0]);
 print("    BS/space/BS echoed.. %s\n",onoff[(opts & EDEL) != 0]);
 print("    Control-H used...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_backspace == 0x08]);
 print("    Mail statements..... %s\n",onoff[!user_record->va_nocharge]); 
 print("    State resident...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_stateres]);
 print("    Name anonymous...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_anonymous]);
 print("    Line delete char.... %02xH\n",user_record->va_linedel);
}
int inp_data(itemnum, user_record)
/* procedure inp_data - query for an item of data to add to the user_record. 
   returns TRUE if input good, else FALSE */
int itemnum;
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 BUFTYPE  buffer;
 int  i;
 extern char *str_skip();
 switch(itemnum) {
case -1: 
 print("modify parameter is not unique.\n");
 break;
case 0:
 print("No such parameter.\n");
 break;
case 1:  /* Username */
 print("Enter codename (11 chars max)? ");
 readline(buffer); 
 reset_parse();
 parse(buffer,buffer);
 buffer[11] = '\0';  /* truncate zero */
 if (valid_hash(buffer) != 0)
  print("Duplicate codename.\n");
 else {
  strcpy(user_record->va_username, buffer);
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break; 
case 2:    /* Password */
 print("Enter password (11 chars max)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 reset_parse();
 parse(buffer,buffer);
 buffer[11] = '\0';
 if (buffer[0] != '\0') {
  hashpassword(buffer,user_record->va_hashpw);
  print("PW update complete.\n");
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 3:  /* Account */
 print("Enter account number (1-255)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i <= 0 || i > 255)
  print("Acct value out of range.\n");
 else {
  if (i == 255)
   print("WARNING! acct set to super-user status.\n");
  user_record->va_useracct = i;
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 4:  /*  echo flag */
 print("Echo characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_ttyoptions &= ~ECHO;
 user_record->va_ttyoptions |= ECHO * getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 5:  /*  edel flag */
 print("Send BS,space,BS to delete characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_ttyoptions &= ~EDEL;
 user_record->va_ttyoptions |= EDEL * getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 6:  /* Control-H */
 print("Control-H instead of DEL key to erase characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_backspace = getflag() ? 0x08 : 0x7F;
 return(TRUE);    
 break;           
                     
case 7:  /* credit balance */
 print("Enter credit adjustment? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i != 0) {
  print("%d + %d = ",user_record->va_creditbalance, i);
  user_record->va_creditbalance += i;
  print("%d cr.\n",user_record->va_creditbalance);
 }
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 8:  /* closed */
 print("Is this account open (yes) ? ");
 user_record->va_closed = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 9:  /* birthdate */
{
 int m, d;
 print("Birthdate month? (1-12)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 m = atoi(buffer);
 print("Birthdate date? (1-31)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 d = atoi(buffer);
 user_record->va_birthdate = (m << 8) | (d << 1);
 return(TRUE);
 break;
}
case 10: /* rating */
 print("Credit rating (1-10)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i != 0) {
  user_record->va_rating = i;
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 11:  /* linedel char */
 print("Enter line-delete code (hex, default 1B)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoh(buffer);
 if (i != 0)
  user_record->va_linedel = i;
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 12:  /* nocharge */
 print("Charge credits to user and mail statement (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_nocharge = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 13: /* name, addr1, addr2, addr3, phone, interests */
{ char         *string, *strbase;
 display(user_record);
 strbase = &user_record->va_stringbuf; /* 192 char buffer */
 print("Full name? ");
 readline(buffer);
    buffer[79] = '\0';
 strcpy(strbase,buffer);
 print("Address line 1? ");
 readline(buffer);
 buffer[79] = '\0';
 string = str_skip(1,strbase);
 strcpy(string,buffer);
 print("Address line 2? ");
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(2,strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);                    
 print("Address line 3? ");
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(3,strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);
 print("Phone number? "); 
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(4, strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);
 string = str_skip(5, strbase);
 i = min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)));
  print("Interests/Hobbies (%d chars max)? ",i);
 readline(buffer);
   buffer[i] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer); 
 return(TRUE);
}
case 14:  /* resident */
  print("Does the user live inside the state (yes)? ");
  user_record->va_stateres = getflag();
  return(TRUE);
  break;
  
case 15: /* anonymous */
 print("Does the user want his real name made public (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_anonymous = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
default:
 print("No such option, try again.");
 break;
}
 return(FALSE);
}
int getflag()
/* procedure getflag - read "yes" and "no" from input and return
   proper boolean value */
{
 BUFTYPE  buf;
 extern reset_parse();
 readline(buf);
 reset_parse();
 parse(buf,buf);
 if (buf[0] != 'n')
  return(TRUE);
 else
  return(FALSE);
}
send_credits(index, numcred)
/* Procedure send_credits - send credit increase to accounting. */
unsigned index;
int   numcred;
{
 struct ac_creditmsg  creditmsg;
 struct ac_creditmsg_reply  creditmsg_reply;
 creditmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 creditmsg.ac_msgnum = CREDITMSG;
 creditmsg.ac_userindex = index;
 creditmsg.ac_credits = numcred;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &creditmsg, &creditmsg_reply,
  sizeof(creditmsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:credit - Unable to send to Acct Admin.\n");
 switch (creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success) {
case 1: print("Credit update complete.  New balance=%d cr\n",
   creditmsg_reply.ac_creditbalance);
        break;  
case 2: print("\007Credit balance overflow!\n");
  break;
default: /* including zero */
  print("\007Error in accounting credit update.\n");
  break;
 }
}
;
 creditmsg.ac_credits = numcred;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &creditmsg, &creditmsg_reply,
  sizeof(creditmsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:credit - Unable to send to Acct Admin.\n");
 switch (creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success) {
case 1: print("Credit update complete.  New balance=%d cr\n",
   creditmsg_reply.ac_creditbalance);

/*  monitor - Central System Terminal Monitor.
  83/09/24. A. E. Klietz.
  This program monitors the activity on all dial-up terminals.
it sleeps for MONSLEEP_SECS seconds and wakes up to see if any
terminal tasks have died due to hangup or fatal program error. When
the monitor discovers a terminal whose taskid reverts back to IDLE,
it forces the modem to hang up the phone by zeroing the DTR bit in the
Modem Control Register.  It also resets the terminal's default 
characteristics and sends a message to the accounting administrator
saying the user logged out.
  Another task of the monitor is to check for no carrier.  If
a terminal loses it's carrier, then the monitor issues a hangup_exception
to the task phone by zeroing the DTR bit in the Modem Control Register
for two seconds.
  When the monitor detects a new carrier, it escapes a GamBit shell
to handle the login procedures.
  The monitor also resets the gl_monitor byte for all other ttys
when a tty that has been monitoring other ttys hangs up.
  The monitor also updates the peak/nonpeak flag.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <io.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/termdef.h"
#include "/def/ac_msgs.h"
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/appdef.h"
#define  EXC_HANGUP  0x0001
#define  EXC_BREAK      0x0002
#define  RUN_BACKGROUND 0x0001
/* flag for setting debug mode. */
/* #define DEBUG */
extern unsigned  My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, My_priority;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
main(argc, argv) 
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 char *version = "1.4";
 int  i, loopcount;
 unsigned taskid; /* id of current running task on device. */
    struct termlist_type *term;
 unsigned msreg; /* modem status register port. */
 FILE *fp;
 sleep(2); /* wait so we dont mix output with foreground task. */
 tprintf("\nCentral Terminal Monitor ver %s.\n",version);
 
 if (argc == 0) {  /* query. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"monitor &  -  initiate terminal monitor.\n");
  exit(0);
 }
 if (My_tid == tty_tid(My_tty))
  abort("fatal:monitor - The monitor must run as a background task.\n");
 if (attach(MONITOR_PORT) != 0)
  abort("fatal:monitor - Another monitor task is already active!\n");
/* The following is the initialization loop for the monitor. */
 for (i=0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
  if (termlist[i].enabled) {
   term = &termlist[i];
   term->taskid = tty_tid(term->ttynum);
   if (term->taskid == -1)  /* what? */
    error("fatal:monitor - nonexistant tty: %d.\n",term->ttynum);    
   if (term->taskid == IDLE)  {
    init_terminal(term);
    setup_stty(term);
   }
  }
  set_glob(); /* set global pointers. */
  check_timeout();  /* set up peak/nonpeak flag */
  tprintf("The GamBit Timeshare System is ready for operations on %s, %s.\n",
   date(), time());
/* The following is the main loop for the monitor. */
 while (TRUE) { /* loop forever. */
  for (i = 0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
   if (termlist[i].enabled) {  /* if disabled then ignore port. */
    term = &termlist[i];
    taskid = tty_tid(term->ttynum);
    if ((taskid == IDLE) && (term->taskid != IDLE)) { /* do hangup processing. */
     term->taskid = IDLE;
     send_accounting_logoff(term);
     setup_stty(term); /* set up login config. */
     tprintf("%s, %s info:monitor - no task for tty%d.\n",
      date(), time(), term->ttynum);
    } /* idle */
    else
     term->taskid = taskid;
    if (term->iobase != 0) /* check carrier change. */
     if ((msreg = io_in(term->iobase+6)) & 0x0008)
      if ((msreg & 0x0080) && (taskid == IDLE)) { /* connected. */
                            if (@&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle) {
tfprintf((fp = fopen(term->name,"wv")),"idle:monitor - system is down.\n");
        fclose(fp);
       }
       else {
/* Start up the GamBit shell so the user can log in to the system. */
        tprintf("%s, %s info:monitor - connection on tty%d.\n",
         date(), time(), term->ttynum);
         escape_tty(term->ttynum, "/cmds/sh");
       }
      }
      else { /* hangup. */
       /* set hangup and thus kill task. */
       set_exception(taskid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);
       tprintf("%s, %s info:monitor - hangup on tty%d.\n",    
        date(), time(), term->ttynum);
      }
   } /* scan */
   if (loopcount >= 20) { /* time to check for timeouts. */
    loopcount = 0;
    check_timeout();
   }
   loopcount++;
   breakable(); /* allow breaks while sleeping. */
   sleep(MONSLEEP_SECS);  /* go to sleep for a few seconds. */
   unbreakable();  /* no interrupts now! */
 } /* loop forever */
 abort("fatal:monitor - unexpected termination.\n");
}
setup_stty(term)
/* Procedure setup_stty - reset the modem to a login configuration. 
 This procedure will set the attributes of the terminal as follows:
  1) Change the tty_RUBOUT to a backspace (0x0008)
  2) Change the line-delete character to control-u.
  3) Disable input gating.
  4) Set full duplex, XON/XOFF, line editing, and tab expanding.
        5) Disconnect phone for modem devices.
  6) set 300 baud 8 bits, 1 stop bit, no parity.
*/
struct termlist_type *term;
{
 FILE *fp;
 struct dev_entry buffer;   /* attribute block buffer. */
 unsigned mcreg; /* modem control register port */
#ifdef DEBUG
 tprintf("debug:monitor - Device attribute set: %s.\n",term->name);
#endif
 fp = fopen(term->name,"wv");
 if (fp == 0) {  /* unable to access modem. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"warn:monitor - Unable to open tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
  return;
 }
 get_attr(fp, &buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) 
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = 0x0008; /* change delete to backspace. */
 else
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = 0x007F; /* delete key. */
 buffer.tty_CANCEL = 0x0015; /* change line-erase to cntl-u. */
 set_attr(fp, &buffer); /* set attributes */
 set_option(fp, get_option(fp) & ~IGATE | ECHO | EDIT | XON_XOFF | ETAB);
 fclose(fp);
 if (term->iobase != 0) {  /* if dial-up port */
  /* force hangup by resetting DTR bit on modem. */
  mcreg = io_in(term->iobase+4);
  io_out(term->iobase+4,mcreg & ~0x0001);
  sleep(2); /* wait for phone to disconnect. */
  io_out(term->iobase+3, 0x83); /* 8 bits, no parity, access baud rate. */
  io_out(term->iobase+0, 0x80); /* lower byte, 300 baud. */
  io_out(term->iobase+1, 0x01); /* upper byte, 300 baud. */
  io_out(term->iobase+3, 0x03); /* deaccess baud rate */
  io_out(term->iobase+4,mcreg);
 }
}
init_terminal(term)                   
/* Procedure init_terminal - initialize port at boot-up.   
This procedure will flush the input and output buffers, and 
set the shell-escape character.  For modems, it also sends
the necessary initialization codes for auto-answering. */
struct termlist_type *term;
{
 FILE *fw, *fr;
 struct dev_entry buffer;
 fw = fopen(term->name, "wv");
 fr = fopen(term->name, "rv");
 if (fr == 0 || fw == 0) { /* unable to open modem. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"warn:monitor - Unable to init tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
  return(0);
 }
/* Before we can initialize the modem, we have to disable echoing, editing
and input gating on the terminal so we don't choke the monitor on an
i/o block.  (argh!)     set_stty will reset the parameters correctly. */
 get_attr(fw,&buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) {
  buffer.tty_ESCAPE = 0x0000; /* disable shell escape. */
  term->iobase = buffer.tty_ioport;
 }
 else {
  buffer.tty_ESCAPE = 0x001A; /* reset escape to cntl-z */
  term->iobase = 0;
 }
 set_attr(fw,&buffer);
 set_option(fw, get_option(fr) & ~ECHO & ~EDIT);
 tfprintf(fw,"%cAT M0E0Q1S0=1S10=10S2=21%c\n",CR,CR); /* initialize Hayes modem. */
 fflush(fw);             
 sleep(5); /* give modem a chance to initialize. */
 while (char_waiting(fr))
  getc(fr); /* flush input buffer */
 fclose(fw);
 fclose(fr);
 tprintf("info:monitor - %s initialized.\n",term->name);
}
send_accounting_logoff(term)
/* Procedure send_accounting_logoff - send a message to the
 Accounting Administrator saying the terminal hung up. */
struct termlist_type *term;
{
    struct ac_logoutmsg logoutmsg;
 struct ac_logoutmsg_reply logoutmsg_reply;
 logoutmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 logoutmsg.ac_msgnum   = LOGOUTMSG;
 logoutmsg.ac_ttynum   = term->ttynum;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &logoutmsg, &logoutmsg_reply,
   sizeof(logoutmsg)) == 0)
  error("fatal:monitor - unable to send to accounting admin.\n");
 if (logoutmsg_reply.ac_logout_success != 1) /* bad logout. */
  tprintf("note:monitor - bad logout request for tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
}
check_timeout()
/* procedure check_timeout - check for 15 minute timeout. */
/* Also, check for peak/non-peak time and set flag as needed. */
{
 register  i;
 struct gl_user_datum  *user_ptr;
 unsigned peak, olddate[2], bindate[2];
 boolean peakchange;
 extern unsigned secdiff();
 get_date(bindate);
 peak = bindate[1] / 3600;
 if (bindate[0] & 0x01)
  peak += 12; /* 24 hour time format */
 peak = (peak < 2 || peak >= 12);  /* true if peak time. */
 peakchange = (peak != @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag);
 
 for(i=0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
  if (termlist[i].enabled) {
   user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[termlist[i].ttynum];
   if (@&user_ptr->gl_userindex != 0 &&  /* must be logged in */
     @&user_ptr->gl_apnum != AP_SYSTEM) { /* dont check system tasks*/
    if (peakchange)
     credit_update(termlist[i].ttynum, 0); 
    /* update credits before changing peak flag */
    olddate[0] = @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[0];
    olddate[1] = @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[1];
    if (secdiff(olddate, bindate) > 900) { /* fifteen minutes */
     set_exception(termlist[i].taskid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);
     tprintf("%s %s info:monitor - timeout on tty%d.\n",
      date(), time(), termlist[i].ttynum);
    }
   }
        }
 if (peakchange) {
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag = peak;
  print("%s, %s info:monitor - start of %speak time.\n",
   date(), time(), (peak ? "" : "non-"));
 }
 get_date(olddate); /* set olddate to 4:40 am */
 olddate[0] &= ~0x01; /* force am */
 olddate[1] = 4*60*60 + 40*60; /* 4:40 */
 if (abs(mindiff(olddate, bindate)) <= 5 && !@&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash) {
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = TRUE;
  create(0, RUN_BACKGROUND, My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh", "/config/down_script",
   0); /* create script to shut down system in 20 minutes. */
 }
}
  
tprintf(format, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6)
/* Procedure tprintf - short form of tfprintf. */
char *format;
int a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6;
{
 tfprintf(stdout, format, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
}
 olddate[1] = 4*60*60 + 40*60; /* 4:40 */
 if (abs(mindiff(olddate, bindate)) <= 5 && !@&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash) {
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = TRUE;
  create(0, RUN_BACKGROUND, My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh", "/config/down_script",
   0); /* create script to shut down system in 20 minutes. l
ption: not a device

/*  sh - user login shell.
  83/09/25. A. E. Klietz.
  'sh' is the login program that replaces the QNX shell
(/cmds/sh) for doing login dialogue with a user.  If 'sh' is
called from another task, then it will transform itself into 
the standard QNX shell, renamed /cmds/qnxsh.  The program is     
activated by pressing cr on the terminal.  It requires a monitor
program to change the shell-escape character to cr beforehand.  
  When activated, this program issues a login header,              
verifies the user, and then transforms itself into the GamBit 
menu program.  
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/globdef.h"
#include "/def/ac_msgs.h"
/*  #define DEBUG  */
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define RUN_CONCURRENT  0x02
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, My_priority;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
main(argc, argv) 
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 if ((Dads_tid != IDLE) || (My_tty == 0)) { /* called from another shell */
  if (argc == 0) { /* query */
   tfprintf(stdout,"use: sh [+s] [+t] [+v] cmd_name [cmd_args]*.\n");
   exit(0);
  }
#ifdef DEBUG
  print("dad: %04x. Me: %04x. My_tty: %d. tty_tid(tty): %04x.\n",
    Dads_tid, My_tid, My_tty, tty_tid(My_tty));
#endif
   trans_QNXshell(argc, argv);
 }
 else  /* set up terminal, do login, and call menu. */
  login_terminal(argc, argv); 
  
 error("sh:fatal - Unexpected termination.\n");
}
login_terminal(argc,argv)
/* Procedure login_dialogue - issue login messages, and verify user. */
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 boolean  validuser = FALSE;
 int   ch, success, trycount = 0;
    unsigned taskid;
    char  username[12];
 struct  user_datum  *user_ptr;
 struct  ac_constmsg  constmsg;
 struct  ac_constmsg_reply constmsg_reply;
 struct  dev_entry  buffer;
 unbreakable();
 set_glob();  /* set global data pointers. */
 constmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 constmsg.ac_msgnum   = CONSTMSG;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT),&constmsg, &constmsg_reply, 
   sizeof(constmsg)) == 0)
  error("fatal:sh - accounting admin failure..\n");
 get_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) {
  print("Press return:\005 ");
  buffer.tty_options &= ~EDIT & ~MAPCR; /* disable line buffering */
  set_attr(stdin, &buffer);
  while ((ch = getchar()) != 0x0D && ch != 0x8D)
   ;  
  if (ch != 0x0D) /* set even parity. */
   io_out(buffer.tty_ioport+3, 0x1A);      
  buffer.tty_options |= EDIT | MAPCR; /* enable line buffering */
  set_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 }
 
 while (char_waiting(stdin)) /* ignore chars before 1st prompt. */
  getchar();
    print("\n  _\n");
 print(" GamBit Timeshare %s\n",
  constmsg_reply.ac_sysloc);
 print("%s, %s  Port: tty%u\n",     
  date(), time(), My_tty);
 print("%s  Tid: %04x.  %s\n",
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? "Peak time." : "non-peak time.",
  My_tid, constmsg_reply.ac_sysnam);
 if (!@&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen)
  print("\007System is closed.\n");
 while (!validuser && (trycount < 4)) {  /* get usernumber/password. */
  trycount++;
  success = getunpw(username);
  if (success != 0)
   set_glob(); /* set terminal characteristics */
  switch (success) {
case 0: if (trycount < 4) /* dont print on last try */
   print("Improper login, try again.\n"); 
  break;
case 1: validuser = TRUE; 
  break;
case 2: print("Sorry, but you have run out of credits for this month.\n\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  hangup();
  break;
case 3: error("fatal:sh - Bad validuz read.\n");
  break;
case 4: print("Your account has been closed, sorry.\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  hangup();
  break;
case 5: print("Your account balance is nearly expired.\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  validuser = TRUE;
  break;
default: error("fatal:sh - unknown login reply.\n");
  break;
  }
 }
 if (!validuser) { /* hangup. */
  print("Invalid access, sorry.\n");
  hangup();  /* force hangup. */
 } 
/* User is now validated. */
    user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[My_tty];
 strcpy_at_from_star(user_ptr->gl_username, username);
 set_user(@&user_ptr->gl_useracct);
 print("Login: %s,  Acct. %03d-%04d.\n", username, @&user_ptr->gl_useracct,
  @&user_ptr->gl_userindex);
 print("Terminal: tty%u, %04x.\n",My_tty, My_tid);
 taskid = create(1, BEQUEATH_TTY, My_priority, 0, 0,
  "/sys/menu", 0);
    error("warn:sh - failed to transform menu.  TID=%x04.\n",
  taskid);
}
int getunpw(username)
/*  Procedure getunpw - obtain usernumber and pw and verify.   
 Returns  0 if bad pw, 1 if ok, 2 if no credits, 3 if bad send. 
 
 It also sets up the global page if 1 (ok) is returned. 
*/
char *username;
{
 BUFTYPE fbuf, namebuf;
 unsigned option_save;
 struct  ac_loginmsg   loginmsg;
 struct  ac_loginmsg_reply  loginmsg_reply;
    struct  dev_entry   buffer;
    loginmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 loginmsg.ac_msgnum   = LOGINMSG;
 loginmsg.ac_ttynum   = My_tty;
 
/*  Copy the device name to the login message, adding a '$'. */
 get_attr(stdout, &buffer);
 loginmsg.ac_ttyname[0] = '$';
 strcpy(&loginmsg.ac_ttyname[1], &buffer.dev_name);
 
 print("Username: ");
 if (readline(fbuf) == EOF)
  exit(0);
 reset_parse();
 upper_parse(fbuf,namebuf);
 namebuf[11] = '\0';  /* truncate to 11+eos characters. */
 strcpy(username, namebuf);
 string_tolower(namebuf); /* convert to lowercase. */
 strcpy(loginmsg.ac_username, namebuf);
 if (parse(fbuf,namebuf) == 0) {
  option_save = get_option(stdin);
  set_option(stdin,option_save & ~ECHO); /* turn off echo */
  print("Password: ");
  if (readline(namebuf) == EOF)
   exit(0);
  set_option(stdin,option_save);
  print("\n");
 }
 string_tolower(namebuf);
 namebuf[11] = '\0';  /* truncate to 11+eos characters. */
 strcpy(loginmsg.ac_userpw, namebuf);
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &loginmsg, &loginmsg_reply,
  sizeof(loginmsg)) == 0)
   error("fatal:sh - accounting admin failure.\n");
 return(loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success);
}
print_renew_msg()
/* procedure print_renew_msg */
{
  print("To renew your account, please mail a check or money order\n");
  print("to:\n\n");
  print("     GamBit Multi Systems, Inc.\n"); 
  print("     P.O. Box 23495\n");
  print("     Minneapolis, MN  55423\n\n");
  print("(Be sure to write your codename somewhere on the check.)\n");
  print("If you have already renewed your account, then please dis-\n");
  print("regard this notice.  Thank you.\n\n");
}
hangup()
/* Procedure hangup - send hangup exception to task. */
{
 set_exception(My_tid, 0x0001, 0);
}
trans_QNXshell(argc, argv)
/* Procedure trans_QNXshell - convert task to a standard shell.  Any
arguments sent to this shell are passed to the QNX shell */
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 unsigned taskid;
 int  i;
 argv[argc] = NULL;
#ifdef DEBUG
 tfprintf(stderr,"qnxsh.\n");
#endif
 
 taskid = create(1, (tty_tid(My_tty) == My_tid) ? BEQUEATH_TTY : RUN_CONCURRENT, 
   My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh",
  argv[1], argv[2], argv[3], argv[4], argv[5], argv[6], argv[7],
  argv[8], argv[9], argv[10], 0);
 tfprintf(stderr,"warn:sh - failed to transform to qnxsh. TID=%x\n",taskid);
 exit(-1);
}
shell.  Any
arguments sent to this shell are 
*
 
~
Copyright (c) 1983, Alan E. Klietz.
System must be shut down before mailing statements.
Line printer is busy.
Enter lower bound of userindexes? 
Enter upper bound of userindexes? 
Bad lower or upper bound.
Ready printer and press return.
Bad valid read on userindex %04u.
userindex %04u empty.
printing finished.
Erasing inactive account: %04u.
error deleting account.
$lpt
wv
Unable to open $lpt.
/jou/%04u
rv
%s - empty or unable to open.
%04u: validuz: %d cr.  and journal: %d cr. do not balance.
Current credit balance = %d cr.
Last login was on: %s, %s.
Current conversion rate is 8 credits per dollar.
 To add credits to your account, please mail a check or money order to:

/*  Mail - mail out billing statements to users.
   83/11/27. A. E. Klietz
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/validef.h"
#include "/def/ac_msgs.h"
#include "/def/jourdef.h"
extern byte My_tty;
main()
{
 char buffer[80];
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 int  upper, lower, i;
 if (attach(ACCOUNTING_PORT) != My_tty)
  error("System must be shut down before mailing statements.\n");
 if (attach(16) != My_tty)
  error("Line printer is busy.\n");
    tfprintf(stdout, "Enter lower bound of userindexes? ");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 lower = atoi(buffer);
 tfprintf(stdout, "Enter upper bound of userindexes? ");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 upper = atoi(buffer);
 if (lower > upper || lower <= 0 || upper <= 0)
  error("Bad lower or upper bound.\n");
 tfprintf(stdout, "Ready printer and press return.\n");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 for (i=lower; i <= upper; i++) {
  if (valid_read(i, &user_record) <= 0)
   error("Bad valid read on userindex %04u.\n",i);
  if (user_record.va_userindex == 0)
   tfprintf(stdout, "userindex %04u empty.\n", i);
  else
   print_stmt(i, &user_record);
 }
 tfprintf(stdout, "printing finished.\n");
}
print_stmt(index, user_record)
unsigned index;
struct  va_user_record  *user_record;
{
 FILE *lp, *fp;
 char name[20];
 struct jr_journal_record journal_record;
 int  lastcr;
 unsigned bindate[2], day1, day2;
 extern char *timeconv(), *dateconv();
 day1 = count_days(user_record->va_lastlogin);
 get_date(bindate);
 day2 = count_days(bindate);
 if (user_record->va_closed || day2 - day1 > 365 || day2 - day1 > 75 &&
  user_record->va_creditbalance == 0) { /* delete account */
  tfprintf(stdout,"Erasing inactive account: %04u.\n",index);
  if (valid_delete(index) == 0)
   error("error deleting account.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (user_record->va_nocharge) /* dont mail statement */
  return(0);
 if ((lp = fopen("$lpt","wv")) == 0)
  error("Unable to open $lpt.\n");
 print_header(lp, index, user_record);
 tsprintf(name, "/jou/%04u", index);  
 if ((fp = fopen(name,"rv")) == 0)
  tfprintf(stdout, "%s - empty or unable to open.\n",name);   
 else {
  lastcr = -32767;
  while (fget(&journal_record, 12, fp) == 12) {
   if (journal_record.jr_entry != NEWAP_ENTRY)
    show_journal(lp, &journal_record);
   if (journal_record.jr_entry != CREDIT_ENTRY)
    lastcr = journal_record.jr_credits;
  }    
     fclose(fp);
 
  if (lastcr != -32767 && lastcr != user_record->va_creditbalance)
   tfprintf(stdout, "\007%04u: validuz: %d cr.  and journal: %d cr. do not balance.\n",
    index, user_record->va_creditbalance, lastcr);
     
  tfprintf(lp,"\014");  /* form feed */
 }
 tfprintf(lp,"\n\nCurrent credit balance = %d cr.\n",
   user_record->va_creditbalance, lastcr);
 tfprintf(lp,"Last login was on: %s, %s.\n\n",dateconv(user_record->va_lastlogin),
  timeconv(user_record->va_lastlogin));
 tfprintf(lp,"Current conversion rate is 8 credits per dollar.\n");
 if (user_record->va_creditbalance < 1000) {
  tfprintf(lp,"\n\n To add credits to your account, please mail a check or money order to:\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"          GamBit Multi Systems\n"); 
  tfprintf(lp,"          P.O. Box 23495\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"          Minneapolis, MN  55423\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"\n(Be sure to write your codename somewhere on the check.)\n");
 }
 tfprintf(lp,"\n\nThank You!\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"\014"); /* form feed */
 fclose(lp);
}
print_header(lp, index, user_record)
FILE *lp;
unsigned index;
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 char *skip_string();
 tfprintf(lp,"\n                G a m B i t    M u l t i   S y s t e m s\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"                            Statement of Account\n\n\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"Codename: %s.  Userindex: %04u.\n",
  user_record->va_username, index);
 tfprintf(lp,"\n%s\n%s\n%s\n%s\n\n",
  user_record->va_stringbuf,
  skip_string(1, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(2, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(3, user_record->va_stringbuf));
}
char *skip_string(count, string)
/* procedure skip_string - skip 'count' strings forward in a list of strings. */
int  count;
char *string;
{
 register   i;
 for (i=1; i<=count; i++) 
  while (*string++ != '\0')
   ;
 return(string);
}

  skip_string(1, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(2, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(3, user_record->va_stringbuf));
}

            (negative if in debt.) */

 int         gl_minute_remainder; /* internal credit_update counter */
    byte  gl_backspace;   /* backspace character. */
 byte  gl_linedel;    /* line-delete character. */
} GL_USER_DATA[NUMDEVICES];

 boolean  gl_warncrash;      /* TRUE if shutdown in progress. */
} GL_SYSTEM_DATA;

/* Validsubs - validation file subroutines.
 83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 
 Hash table added  83/12/25. 
 This is a set of library procedures for manipulating the
/val/validuz and /val/valindx files.  
 The major routines contained are:
 valid_hash(username) - return userindex for username.
         returns 0 if not found.
 valid_read(userindex, &user_record) - read data into user_record.
      returns 0 if bad read.
 valid_write(userindex, &user_record) - write user_record to file.
      returns 0 if bad write.
 
 valid_create(username) - returns new userindex to open space on file.
      returns 0 if no room, 32767 if duplicate entry.
 valid_delete(userindex) - deletes the entry from valindx.
      returns 0 if already empty.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "/def/aekdef.h"
#include "/def/validef.h"
#define  HASHSEED   0xD16B
int hash_index(username)
char *username;
/* procedure hash_index - return a value from 0 to 255 based on 
'username' */
{
 
 int  hash;
 register i;
 hash = HASHSEED;  /* set inital value */  
 for (i=0; username[i] != '\0'; i++)   /* scramble */
  hash = ((hash * username[i]) >> 3);
 return(hash & 0xFF);  /* truncate to 8 bits. */
}
hash_read(fp, user_hash, indx, curloc)
FILE  *fp;
struct  vi_user_hash *user_hash;
int   indx, *curloc;
/* Procedure hash_read - read index-th record from valindx file.
File is assumed already open.  Curloc keeps track of the current 
file position. */
{
 register i;
 i = *curloc;
 *curloc = (indx-1)*16 + 512;
 fiseek(fp, *curloc - i);
 *curloc += 16;
 if (fget(user_hash, 16, fp) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - hash index outside of file!\n");
}
hash_write(fp, user_hash, indx, curloc)
FILE  *fp;
struct  vi_user_hash *user_hash;
int   indx, *curloc;
/* Procedure hash_write - write index-th record from valindx file.
File is assumed already open.  Curloc keeps track of the current 
file position. */
{
 register i;
 i = *curloc;
 *curloc = (indx-1)*16 + 512;
 fiseek(fp, *curloc - i);
 *curloc += 16;
 if (fput(user_hash, 16, fp) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - unable to write valindx record.\n");
}
unsigned valid_hash(username)
/* Procedure valid_hash - search the /val/valindx file for a userindex
   to match the given username.   Returns zero if not found. */
char *username;
{
 FILE *fp;  /* pointer to index file. */
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash;
 int   hashtable[256];
 int  hash, curloc;
 if (sizeof(user_hash) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - user_hash record is not 16 length!\n");
 if (username[0] == '\0')
  return(0);  /* blank username */
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/valindx", "rv")) == 0)
  return(0);  /* unable to open valindx file. */
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) < 512)  
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");
 curloc = 512;
 hash = hashtable[hash_index(username)];
 if (hash == 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hash, &curloc);
 
 for (;;) {
  if (equal(user_hash.vi_username, username))
   break;
  if (user_hash.vi_link == 0) {
   fclose(fp);
   return(0); /* name not found */
  }
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, user_hash.vi_link, &curloc);
 } 
 fclose(fp);
 if (user_hash.vi_userindex == 0) 
  error("valid:warn - user index is zero?\n");
 return(user_hash.vi_userindex);
}
unsigned valid_read(userindex, user_record)
/* Procedure valid_read - read in the user_record pointed by userindex. 
Returns zero if bad read or not found. */
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;  /* pointer to buffer. */
{
 FILE *fp;  /* pointer to valid file. */
 if (sizeof(struct va_user_record) != 256) 
  error("valid:warn - user_record is not 256 length!\n");
 if (userindex <= 0 || userindex >= 128) {
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - userindex out of bounds. ui=%d.\n",userindex);
  return(0);
 }
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/validuz","rv")) == 0)
  return(0);  /* file not found */
 if (fiseek(fp, (userindex-1)*256) != 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - offset outside of valid file!\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (fget(user_record, 256, fp) != 256) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - user_record read past EOF.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 fclose(fp);
 return(1); /* signal good read. */
}
unsigned valid_write(userindex, user_record)
/* Procedure valid_write - write a user_record to the valid file at userindex. */
unsigned userindex;
struct     va_user_record *user_record; /* pointer to buffer */
{
 FILE *fp; /* pointer to valid file. */
    if (userindex <= 0 || userindex >= 128) {
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - userindex write out of bounds.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/validuz", "rwv")) == 0)
  return(0);
 if (fiseek(fp, (userindex-1)*256) != 0) {  
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - write offset outside of valid file!\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (fput(user_record, 256, fp) != 256) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - write error.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 fclose(fp);
 return(1); /* signal good write. */
}
unsigned valid_create(username)
/* Procedure valid_create - create an entry in the valindx file and
the valid file. Entries are inserted into a 512 byte hash table that
heads the /val/valindx file. */
char *username;
{
 FILE *fp, *fp2;
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash;
 int  numread, tableloc, indexloc, recordloc;
 int    hashtable[256];
 
 if (username[0] == '\0' || valid_hash(username) != 0) 
  return(32767);  /* blank name or duplicate name error. */
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/valindx","rwv")) == 0)   
  return(0);
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) != 512)
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");
 indexloc = 1; /* find open hole in /val/valindx */
 while ((numread = fget(&user_hash, 16, fp)) == 16 &&
   user_hash.vi_username[0] != '\0') 
  indexloc++;
 if (numread == 16)
  fiseek(fp, -16); /* backspace 1 record */
 
 if ((fp2 = fopen("/val/validuz", "rwv")) == 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 recordloc = 1;
 while ((numread = fget(&user_record, 256, fp2)) == 256 && 
   user_record.va_userindex != 0)
  recordloc++;
 
 if (numread == 256)  /* found empty slot. */
  fiseek(fp2, -256); /* backspace one record. */
 zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));
 strcpy(user_record.va_username, username);
 user_record.va_userindex = recordloc;
 fput(&user_record, 256, fp2); /* write new record. */
 fclose(fp2);
 zero(&user_hash, sizeof(user_hash));
 strcpy(user_hash.vi_username, username);
 user_hash.vi_userindex = recordloc;
 user_hash.vi_link = 0;
 fput(&user_hash, 16, fp); 
 tableloc = hash_index(username);
 rewind(fp); 
 if (hashtable[tableloc] == 0) {  /* no collision */
  hashtable[tableloc] = indexloc;
  fput(hashtable, 512, fp); /* rewrite hash table */
 }
 else { /* collision */
  int  curloc;
  curloc = 0;
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hashtable[tableloc], &curloc);
  while (user_hash.vi_link != 0) 
   hash_read(fp, &user_hash, user_hash.vi_link, &curloc);   
  fiseek(fp, -16); /* backup 1 record */
  user_hash.vi_link = indexloc;  
  fput(&user_hash, 16, fp);
 }
 
 fclose(fp);
 return(recordloc);
 
}
unsigned valid_delete(userindex)
/* Procedure valid_delete - delete an entry from the valid file and
zero out its valindx entry. */
unsigned userindex;
{
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash, user_hash2;
 FILE *fp;
 int  prevlink, curloc, hashindex, index;
 int     hashtable[256];
 
 if (valid_read(userindex, &user_record) == 0)
  return(0);
 
 hashindex = hash_index(user_record.va_username);
 index = user_record.va_userindex;
                                
 zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));
 if (valid_write(userindex, &user_record) == 0)
  return(0);
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/valindx","rwv")) == 0)
  return(0);
 
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) != 512)
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");
                  
 if (hashtable[hashindex] == 0)
  error("valid:fatal - hash entry is zero.\n");
 curloc = 512;
 if (hashtable[hashindex] == index) {
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, index, &curloc);
  hashtable[hashindex] = user_hash.vi_link;
  rewind(fp);
  fput(hashtable, 512, fp);
  curloc = 512;
 }
 else {
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hashtable[hashindex], &curloc);
  prevlink = hashtable[hashindex];
  while (user_hash.vi_link != index) {
   prevlink = user_hash.vi_link;
   hash_read(fp, &user_hash, prevlink, &curloc);
  }
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash2, index, &curloc); 
  user_hash.vi_link = user_hash2.vi_link;
  hash_write(fp, &user_hash, prevlink, &curloc);
 }
 user_hash.vi_username[0] = '\0';
 user_hash.vi_userindex = 0;
 user_hash.vi_link = 0;
 hash_write(fp, &user_hash, index, &curloc);
 fclose(fp);
 return(userindex);  /* success */
}
htable[hashindex],
